- **Escape** — `OnFilterBoxKeyDown` on the filter box clears `FilterText` and calls `FocusSidebarTree()`, which focuses a real `TreeViewItem` (selected row, else the first realized row) — the `TreeView` container itself doesn't accept keyboard focus, it delegates to items (gotcha #138).

**Removed** (type-ahead): View `_typeAheadBuffer`/`_typeAheadAnchor`/`_typeAheadResetTimer`/`OnSidebarTypeAhead*`/`ResetTypeAhead`/`SetNodeExpanded` + the reset-on-click hook; VM `ResolveTypeAheadAsync`/`BuildFullTypeAheadIndexAsync`/`AddTypeAheadNode`/`NodeSearchText`/`FindFirstMatch`/`FindFromAnchor`/`FindAnchorIndex`/`TypeAheadEntry`/`TypeAheadResult`/`PrimeNameCacheForTest`; the type-ahead unit tests in `TreePerfTests` + the type-ahead probe. **Kept**: the session name cache + the whole filter (`ApplyFilterAsync`/`ApplyFilterToGroup`/`CountMatches`/lazy COUNT load).

**Tests**: [TreePerfTests.cs](tests/EmberTern.Tests/TreePerfTests.cs) trimmed to filter/count/lazy-load only; new headless probe `ConnectionExpandBindingProbe.TypeToFilter_TreeTyping_RedirectsToFilterBox` proves all three behaviours against the real `MainWindow` — (a) typing while a tree item is focused fills the filter + moves focus there, (b) Ctrl+F focuses the filter, (c) Escape clears it + moves focus off the box.

**Gotcha — promote to architecture lore.**

138. **The Avalonia `TreeView` container does not accept keyboard focus — focus a `TreeViewItem` instead.** `tree.Focus()` is a no-op in practice (and headless-provably so: the focused element stays unchanged); the TreeView delegates keyboard focus to its item containers. To "return focus to the tree", focus a real `TreeViewItem` — the selected row's container (`FindTreeViewItemFor(tree, tree.SelectedItem)`) if there is a selection, else the first realized `TreeViewItem` descendant. Corollary for routed-event handlers attached to the TreeView: input only routes through the TreeView (so a tunnel handler on it fires) when a descendant **item** is focused — which is the normal case after a click, and the basis of the type-to-filter redirect.

### TreeView limitation + potential TreeDataGrid migration (observation, parked)

The sidebar is still an Avalonia `TreeView` with a nested `VirtualizingStackPanel` (`TreeView.ItemsPanel` + `TreeViewItem.ItemsPanel`). Avalonia estimates the outer scroll extent across the nested VSPs, so when a single category with thousands of children is expanded the scrollbar thumb is mis-sized and the position can jump — a **control-level limitation we cannot fix from our code**, only mitigate by keeping fewer rows realized (which lazy-load + COUNT-only does). If, after living with Etap 1, the scroll behaviour on very large expanded categories is still unacceptable, the cure is migrating the sidebar to **`TreeDataGrid`** (`HierarchicalTreeDataGridSource` flattens the visible rows into ONE virtualized stream → accurate extent, smooth scroll, 100k+ rows). Migration cost (medium-large): verify the `Avalonia.Controls.TreeDataGrid` NuGet's compat with Avalonia 12.0.x, rewrite the per-type templates (mixed Folder/Connection/Metadata node types), drag&drop (gotcha #40), context menus, selection wiring, and expand-state persistence. Parked as its own architectural sprint — measure first (`EMBERTERN_PERF_DIAG=1`), migrate only if the scroll is still a real problem.

### Trigger Editor (Easy mode) — dedicated 4-tab surface (shipped 2026-06-20)

Triggers now open in a dedicated **Trigger Detail** experience consistent with the View / Procedure editors: four tabs — **Editor** (Source ⇄ Easy + Compile) · **Description** (editable `COMMENT ON TRIGGER`) · **Dependencies** · **DDL** (read-only). Easy mode is the IBExpert improvement over a raw trigger editor: the trigger metadata (Table / Timing / Events / Position / Active) as a structured header + an **editable Variables grid** above a body-only editor — variable declarations get a real grid instead of hand-typed `DECLARE`s. **1396 → 1459 tests** (+63). Build clean (0/0), 8s smoke OK.

**Architecture decision — a focused `TriggerDetailTabViewModel`, reusing the procedure body machinery.** A trigger *body* is structurally identical to a procedure body (`DECLARE … BEGIN … END`), so the body/variables layer is **reused wholesale** — `ProcedureBodySplitter.Split` (Source→model) + `DdlGenerator.BuildProcedureBody` (model→Source) + `ProcedureVariableRowViewModel`. Only the trigger **header** is genuinely different (no params/returns; instead `FOR table`, timing, events, position, active), so it gets its own bounded `TriggerSignatureParser` + `DdlGenerator.BuildCreateOrAlterTrigger`. No new UX pattern: the editor uses the same unified 5-section toolbar (mode toggle · Compile · collection +/−/↑↓ routed to Variables · Format/Comment/Uncomment · Close), the same dirty-tracking + WorkGuard, the same `SqlEditorBehavior` (autocomplete/Ctrl-click), the same shared `SqlFormatter`, the same persistence shape, and the same dependency-tree templates.

**Reuse (directly):** `ProcedureBodySplitter` + `DdlGenerator.BuildProcedureBody`; `ProcedureVariableRowViewModel` (via a new **`IFieldRowOwner`** interface — two implementers now, Procedure + Trigger VMs, so the interface is justified; `ProcedureFieldRowBase` retargeted from `ProcedureDetailTabViewModel?` to `IFieldRowOwner?`); the field-grid column builder extracted to a shared **`Views/FieldGridColumns.cs`** (Procedure view now calls it too — no duplication); `SqlFormatter` (Alt+F + toolbar); `FirebirdDdlReader.FetchDdlAsync(Trigger)` for the read-only DDL tab; `TableDetailTabViewModel.BuildDependencyTree` / `MapObjectTypeToKind`; `DdlGenerator.BuildRelationComment` (→ `BuildCommentTrigger` = `COMMENT ON TRIGGER`); the `NumericUpDown` `decimal?` bridge (gotcha #57) for Position; the FK dialog's `ComboBox`+`IsTextSearchEnabled` searchable-table picker pattern.

**New code:**
- [TriggerSignatureParser.cs](src/EmberTern.Core/Sql/TriggerSignatureParser.cs) — parses `{CREATE [OR ALTER] | ALTER | RECREATE} TRIGGER name [ACTIVE|INACTIVE] FOR table [ACTIVE|INACTIVE] {BEFORE|AFTER} event[ OR …] [POSITION n] AS body` → `{name, table, timing, events, position, active, body}`. Accepts ACTIVE/INACTIVE in either position (the reader emits `[INACTIVE] FOR table`; the canonical form is `FOR table ACTIVE …`). Fails (→ keep last-good model + notice, like the procedure parser) on a DB-level trigger / malformed header.
- [TriggerHeaderInfo.cs](src/EmberTern.Core/Metadata/TriggerHeaderInfo.cs) + `DdlGenerator.BuildCreateOrAlterTrigger` / `BuildTriggerName` / `BuildCommentTrigger`.
- [FirebirdDdlReader](src/EmberTern.Firebird/FirebirdDdlReader.cs): `FetchTriggerSourceAsync` (editable `CREATE OR ALTER TRIGGER`) + `FetchTriggerBodyAsync` (the DECLARE…BEGIN…END after AS) + `StripLeadingAs` (gotcha #139). `BuildTriggerDdlAsync` now strips a leading `AS` from `RDB$TRIGGER_SOURCE` — fixes a latent **double-`AS`** bug for databases that store the source beginning with `AS`.
- [FirebirdTableDetailReader](src/EmberTern.Firebird/FirebirdTableDetailReader.cs): `GetTriggerHeaderAsync` (decodes `RDB$TRIGGER_TYPE` via new `DecodeTriggerHeader` = timing `& 1` + `DecodeTriggerOps`) · `GetTriggerDescriptionAsync` · `GetTriggerDependenciesAsync` (RDB$DEPENDENCIES type 2).
- [TriggerDetailTabViewModel.cs](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/TriggerDetailTabViewModel.cs) + [TriggerDetailTabView.axaml(.cs)](src/EmberTern.App/Views/TriggerDetailTabView.axaml).

**Auto-naming** — `{TABLE}_{B|A}{I?}{U?}{D?}_{position}` (e.g. `ORDERS_BIUD_50`, `CUSTOMERS_AU_100`). Regenerates from {table, timing, events, position} for a **new** trigger until the user types a name (then sticks — the FK-wizard `_lastAutoName`/`_userOverrodeName` pattern, gotcha #66); never overwrites an existing trigger's name (read-only — Firebird can't rename via CREATE OR ALTER). The spec's first example `CUSTOMERS_AIU_100` was a typo for INSERT+UPDATE+DELETE → the mapping table is authoritative → `CUSTOMERS_AIUD_100`.

**Wiring** ([MainWindowViewModel](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/MainWindowViewModel.cs), [WorkspaceTabViewModel](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/WorkspaceTabViewModel.cs), [WorkspaceState](src/EmberTern.Core/Workspace/WorkspaceState.cs), [MainWindow.axaml](src/EmberTern.App/Views/MainWindow.axaml)) — strictly parallel to the Procedure path: `OpensAsTriggerDetail` predicate + `CreateTriggerDetail` factory (loads domains + tables best-effort) + `OnOpenDdlRequested` branch + dedup-kind + `SelectTab` lazy-load + native `CoreTabKind.TriggerDetail` persistence (Snapshot/Load) + `NewTrigger` command (`Icon.TriggerPlus`, starts in Easy with BEFORE INSERT defaults) + `OnTriggerCreated` (refresh tree, close New tab, reopen real trigger) + `IsTriggerDetailTabActive`/`ActiveTriggerDetail` + the toolbar router cases (`ActiveCollection` → Variables) + `ShowTransactionButtons`/`IsClosableTabActive`/`ShowModeSection`/`ShowHelperSection` + `TriggerEasyModePreference` (global, Capture/Restore) + `CanCreateTrigger` re-notify on connect. New `WorkspaceTabKind.TriggerDetail` in both the VM and Core persistence enums.

**Cursors/subprograms in a trigger body** are preserved verbatim through the round-trip (`_preservedCursors`/`_preservedSubprograms` re-emitted by `BuildBodyModel`) but not surfaced as editable grids — the spec asks for a Variables tab only; a trigger rarely declares them, and they're never dropped on Compile.

**Tests** — [TriggerSignatureParserTests](tests/EmberTern.Tests/TriggerSignatureParserTests.cs) (parse forms incl. reader-emitted INACTIVE-before-FOR, multi-event, quoted idents, ALTER/RECREATE, fail cases), [TriggerDdlGeneratorTests](tests/EmberTern.Tests/TriggerDdlGeneratorTests.cs) (auto-name mapping theory, CREATE OR ALTER shape, validation throws, COMMENT), [TriggerDetailTests](tests/EmberTern.Tests/TriggerDetailTests.cs) (Source→Easy, full round-trip, Compile reassembly, auto-name + override-sticks + existing-no-fire, dirty/WorkGuard, mode-toggle-not-an-edit, SelectedTable null-clobber guard), [TriggerReaderTests](tests/EmberTern.Tests/TriggerReaderTests.cs) (`StripLeadingAs`, `DecodeTriggerHeader`).

**DB behaviour is user-verified** (no live FB in this environment): unit tests pin the parser/generator/VM/decode logic; the actual connect → open trigger → Easy/Source → Compile flow is the user's smoke check, consistent with the codebase's DB-path precedent. On branch **`feat/trigger-editor`** — NOT merged to master (awaiting user approval).

**Gotcha — promote to architecture lore.**

139. **`RDB$TRIGGER_SOURCE` may or may not begin with the `AS` keyword — it's database/tool-dependent.** Some Firebird databases store the trigger source starting at `AS` (`AS\nDECLARE…BEGIN…END`), others store just the body (`DECLARE…BEGIN…END` or `BEGIN…END`). A DDL reconstructor that unconditionally re-adds `AS` before the source produces a double `AS` for the first kind (a latent bug in EmberTern's trigger DDL until this milestone). Strip a single leading `AS` word from the blob (`FirebirdDdlReader.StripLeadingAs`, word-boundary-aware) before re-emitting / before splitting the body into the Easy model. Contrast with `RDB$PROCEDURE_SOURCE`, which is reliably the body alone (gotcha #114) — triggers are the irregular one.

### Trigger Editor — pre-merge fixes (shipped 2026-06-20)

Six fixes after live-DB testing of the Trigger Editor. **1459 → 1467 tests** (+8). Build clean (0/0), 8s smoke OK. Still on `feat/trigger-editor` (not merged).

**1. BLOCKER — compile of a simple trigger failed with "Unexpected end of command".** Root cause was in [FirebirdDdlExecutor.SplitStatements](src/EmberTern.Firebird/FirebirdDdlExecutor.cs), the splitter that breaks a multi-statement DDL payload into one-statement-per-`FbCommand`. It split on any top-level `;` (block-depth 0), but a trigger/procedure's **`DECLARE VARIABLE …;` sits BEFORE the `BEGIN`** — at depth 0 — so it cut the trigger mid-DECLARE and sent a truncated `CREATE … AS DECLARE VARIABLE ID_NAGL T_ID` to the server. (Latent for procedures with a DECLARE section too — the autoincrement trigger uses `AS BEGIN …` so it never hit it.) Rewrote the splitter to be **PSQL-aware**: a `CREATE/ALTER/RECREATE` of a `PROCEDURE/TRIGGER/FUNCTION/PACKAGE` is scanned as ONE unit — phase 1 (before `AS`) skips balanced parens (so an `AS` inside `CAST(x AS y)` / a param list isn't the separator) and treats a top-level `;` as a no-body terminator (UDR/EXTERNAL); phase 2 (after `AS`) ignores all `;` and ends at the `END` closing the outermost `BEGIN` (CASE-nested, string/comment-aware, with a peek past a FB3 subprogram's `END`). Plain DDL/DML (CREATE TABLE, CREATE VIEW … AS SELECT, ALTER TABLE, …) still splits on top-level `;`. The managed driver runs a `CREATE TRIGGER` with internal `;` as one command — no SET TERM needed — so sending it whole compiles. (gotcha #140). 9 new splitter tests incl. the user's exact failing trigger.

**2. OLD/NEW IntelliSense in the trigger body.** `NEW.` / `OLD.` now complete the trigger's table columns (only that table — not global). [SqlCompletionController](src/EmberTern.App/Completion/SqlCompletionController.cs) gained an optional `contextTableProvider`; when the dot qualifier is `NEW`/`OLD` and a provider is set, it resolves to that table (reusing the existing column-cache/`EnsureColumnsAsync` path), else falls back to the FROM/alias resolver. [SqlEditorBehavior.Attach](src/EmberTern.App/Completion/SqlEditorBehavior.cs) takes the provider; the trigger view passes `() => _currentVm?.TableName` for its source + body editors. Non-trigger editors pass null (NEW/OLD have no meaning there).

**3. SearchablePicker — one shared filterable lookup.** Audit: every picker was `ComboBox` + `IsTextSearchEnabled` (type-to-**jump** only, no filtering) — painful on big lists (tables 2356, generators 1544, domains 160). New [Controls/SearchablePicker.cs](src/EmberTern.App/Controls/SearchablePicker.cs) — an `AutoCompleteBox` preconfigured for case-insensitive **contains-filter** + `MinimumPrefixLength=0` + show-all-on-focus (expands like a combo, filters as you type). Migrated the **standalone big-list** pickers: Trigger→Table, FK dialog→Referenced table, AddFieldDialog→Domain (+ItemTemplate via `ValueMemberBinding`) + Generator. **Kept as ComboBox** (documented): in-cell DataGrid Type/Domain combos (NewTable / TableDetail / FieldGridColumns — a filtered popup inside an edit cell is a separate, risky change; they retain type-ahead) and tiny fixed enums (timing/FK-actions/table-kind/charset/dialect/BLOB-subtype/basic-type ≤14, where filtering is pointless).

**4. Trigger auto-name format** `{TABLE}_{B|A}{events}{position}` — position glued on with no extra underscore: `STANMAG_BU99`, `ORDERS_BIUD50` (was `STANMAG_BU_99`). [DdlGenerator.BuildTriggerName](src/EmberTern.Core/Metadata/DdlGenerator.cs).

**5. Global UPPERCASE policy for object names** (gotcha #141). User-typed object names are coerced to UPPERCASE on input (Firebird folds unquoted identifiers; EmberTern keeps names uppercase consistently). Applied to the editable name fields of **Trigger** (`EditableTriggerName`), **Procedure** (`EditableProcedureName`), **View** (`EditableViewName`) via the established `_settingNameUpper` re-entrancy-guarded setter (gated on `!_suppressDirty` so ctor/load/parse don't coerce). **New Table + field/variable names already uppercased** (Sesja 1 + `ProcedureFieldRowBase`). Generators / exceptions / functions have **no dedicated creators** (created via raw SQL in the editor) → nothing to coerce there; if they get creators later they adopt the same pattern.

**6. Trigger toolbar — removed transaction buttons.** A trigger is never run manually and its Compile auto-commits in an autonomous DDL tx (Phase A), so there's no working transaction to Commit/Rollback from a trigger tab. `ShowTransactionButtons` no longer includes `IsTriggerDetailTabActive` — the trigger toolbar is mode-toggle · Compile · collection (Variables) · Format/Comment/Uncomment · Close. (Triggers never had Execute — that's procedure-only.)

**Gotchas — promote to architecture lore.**

140. **DDL statement splitting must treat a PSQL `CREATE/ALTER/RECREATE {PROCEDURE|TRIGGER|FUNCTION|PACKAGE}` as one unit — its DECLARE-section semicolons are at block-depth 0, BEFORE the BEGIN.** A BEGIN/END-depth splitter (gotchas #55/#128) correctly keeps body semicolons together but still cuts at a top-level `DECLARE VARIABLE …;` because that `;` precedes the first BEGIN (depth 0) → the server gets a truncated `CREATE … AS DECLARE VARIABLE x` → "Unexpected end of command". The fix is statement-shape detection: when a statement starts with a PSQL header, scan it whole — skip to `AS` (skipping balanced parens so `CAST(x AS y)` doesn't false-match), then ignore all `;` and terminate at the `END` that closes the outermost `BEGIN` (CASE-aware; peek past a subprogram's `END`); a header `;` before any `AS`/`BEGIN` terminates a no-body declaration (UDR/EXTERNAL). Views (`CREATE VIEW … AS SELECT`) are NOT PSQL — they split on `;`. The managed FbCommand runs a single CREATE-with-internal-`;` fine (no SET TERM); the splitter only needs to avoid over-splitting.

141. **EmberTern coerces user-typed object names to UPPERCASE on input — the one consistent convention.** Firebird folds unquoted identifiers to uppercase, so a name entered as `stanmag_bu99` becomes `STANMAG_BU99` in the catalog anyway; coercing in the editor keeps the displayed name honest and the DB free of accidental mixed-case (which would require quoting forever). The pattern: a `_settingNameUpper` re-entrancy flag + uppercase-and-reassign in the `[ObservableProperty]` change handler, gated on `!_suppressDirty` so programmatic ctor/load/parse sets aren't coerced (and the field is read-only for existing objects anyway). Applied to every editable object-name field (Trigger/Procedure/View names; New Table name; field/variable names). Any future object creator (generator/exception/function/etc.) MUST adopt the same handler — don't leave a creator that writes a lowercase name.

### SearchableComboBox — general filtering selector + Faza 1 wiring (shipped 2026-06-20)

Decision (user): the in-grid pickers from the prior milestone went the wrong way — **Type is a small closed dictionary and must stay a plain `ComboBox`**; only large dictionaries (domains, tables, views, procedures, generators, functions) get a searchable picker. `SearchablePicker` (a bare `AutoCompleteBox`) was rejected as the searchable control because it has no dropdown chevron, the `"(none)"` sentinel was clumsy, and it could clear the value on partial-typed text. Built a dedicated control instead. **Researched first** (user gate): Avalonia 12.0.0 `ComboBox` has no `IsEditable`, no built-in filterable/tabbed selector, and the third-party `PoulLorca/AvaloniaSearchableComboBox` is single-list / `ToString`-filter with no tabs — so a custom control is justified.

**`SearchableComboBox` (new, `EmberTern.App.Controls`) — the app's standard picker for large dictionaries.** A `TemplatedControl`: closed = display text + ✕ clear + chevron; open = **top-level popup** (separate `PopupRoot` → never clipped by the AvaloniaEdit body editor, fixes the popup-under-editor bug) with an **in-popup filter box** and a rich, multi-column list with an optional column header. **Commit ONLY on an explicit pick (click a row / Enter) — never on focus loss** (user requirement; avoids accidental changes on big lists), so binding `SelectedItem` is null-safe (no guarded wrapper needed). Empty = empty field (**no `"(none)"` sentinel**; clear via ✕). Designed with a `Sections` (tabs) API for **Domain | Table column** from the start (renders a `TabControl` when ≥1 section; single list otherwise) — Faza 4 lights up the Table-column tab. Popup content is built in code for template robustness. `MaxDropDownHeight` default 360 (easy to raise). Files: [SearchableComboBox.cs](src/EmberTern.App/Controls/SearchableComboBox.cs), [SearchableComboBoxSection.cs](src/EmberTern.App/Controls/SearchableComboBoxSection.cs), [Themes/SearchableComboBox.axaml](src/EmberTern.App/Themes/SearchableComboBox.axaml) (ControlTheme, registered in App.axaml), new `Icon.ChevronDown` + `Icon.Search` geometries. Pure `FilterItems`/`DisplayText` helpers are unit-tested; a headless probe proves the control templates + open/select/clear/close without throwing (the visual filtering UX is the user's live smoke).

**Faza 1 wiring (Type→ComboBox, Domain→SearchableComboBox).** In `FieldGridColumns` (Procedure Input/Output/Variables + Trigger Variables), `NewTableTabView`, and `TableDetailTabView` Pola: **Type reverted to plain `ComboBox`** (type-ahead, no filter), **Domain → `SearchableComboBox`** (single section) bound to `SelectedDomainSpec` with `DisplayMemberPath="Name"` and a shared `DomainRowTemplate` ([Themes/PickerTemplates.axaml](src/EmberTern.App/Themes/PickerTemplates.axaml) — one definition Faza 2 will enrich to `Name | Type | Size | Scale | Not Null`). FieldGridColumns resolves that template from app resources so code + XAML share it. The `"(none)"` sentinel was removed from all 4 grid VMs; `SelectedDomainSpec` getters/setters simplified to empty→null / null=clear (the picker commits only on explicit pick/clear, so the old null-clobber guards are gone). **Regression fix for req #3:** with Type back as a ComboBox, the disabled cell shows the domain's resolved type immediately (`SyncTypeDisplayFromDomain` mirrors Type/Size/Scale for Procedure/Trigger; NewTable shows it via its resolved-type TextBlock) — fixes the exact "Type blank after picking a domain" bug (`AutoCompleteBox` didn't render a programmatically-set `SelectedItem`). TableDetail keeps the field's type visible (full domain-resolved display is Faza 3; never blank).

**Still on `SearchablePicker`** (to migrate next, before/with Faza 2 for full consistency): AddFieldDialog Domain + Generator, ForeignKeyDialog ref-table, TriggerDetailTabView header Table. `SearchablePicker.cs` will be deleted once those move to `SearchableComboBox`.

**Tests: 1472 → 1481** (+8 filter helpers, +1 headless template probe; sentinel tests rewritten to the clear-via-✕ behaviour). Build 0/0, smoke OK. Two commits: `29fb132` (control foundation), Faza 1 wiring (this).

**Gotcha — promote to architecture lore.**

142. **Avalonia `AutoCompleteBox` does not render a programmatically-set `SelectedItem` (and has no dropdown chevron) — don't use it as an in-cell selector that must show a value set from code.** When a domain is picked, the VM mirrors the resolved base type into the (disabled) Type cell; an `AutoCompleteBox` bound to that value showed BLANK because it only syncs its `Text` from user typing / dropdown pick, not from a code-side `SelectedItem` change — the "Type blank after picking a domain" regression. A plain `ComboBox` reflects a programmatic `SelectedItem` immediately. Rule: for a small closed list that must display a code-set selection, use `ComboBox`; for a large dictionary that needs filtering + a chevron + commit-only semantics, use the custom `SearchableComboBox` (which builds its own popup + display and commits only on explicit pick). `AutoCompleteBox` is the wrong tool for both.

### SearchableComboBox — Faza 4 foundation (Table-column picker control) (shipped 2026-06-20)

First, reusable half of Faza 4 (A=5a merge, B=B1 two-pane), built so the working Domain picker (Faza 1–3) is untouched — the grids are NOT changed yet, app fully working. The merge + per-owner data wiring is the next step.

- **`ColumnRef`** ([ColumnRef.cs](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/ColumnRef.cs)) — a TYPE OF COLUMN source: `Qualified` (`TABLE.COLUMN`) + `TypeOfClause` (`COLUMN TABLE.COLUMN`, stored in a field's TypeOf; `ComposeType` prepends `TYPE OF`).
- **`IColumnsLoader`** ([IColumnsLoader.cs](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/IColumnsLoader.cs)) — `LoadColumnsAsync(table)`; field-row owners implement it (lazy per-table column load — never eager-loads every column of every table).
- **`SearchableComboBox` custom-content extension (additive).** New `ISearchableComboBoxContent { Action<object?>? CommitRequested }` + `SearchableComboBoxSection.Content` (a `Control`). When a section sets `Content`, the host renders it instead of the built-in filter+list, wires its commit, and **hides the shared filter box for that tab** (custom content self-filters). The Domain section (built-in list) path is unchanged.
- **`TableColumnPicker`** ([TableColumnPicker.cs](src/EmberTern.App/Controls/TableColumnPicker.cs)) — the B1 two-pane Table-column tab: filterable table list + the selected table's columns (lazy via `IColumnsLoader`), picking a column commits a `ColumnRef`. Implements `ISearchableComboBoxContent`. Reuses `SearchableComboBox.FilterItems`.

**Tests: 1496 → 1497** (+1 ColumnRef; control/picker behaviour is the live smoke). Build 0/0, smoke OK.

### SearchableComboBox — Faza 4 Krok 1 (Procedure/Trigger merged Domain/Column picker) (shipped 2026-06-20)

Krok 1 of Faza 4 (Decision A=5a merge, B=B1 two-pane). The **Procedure/Trigger** field grids (Input/Output params + Variables, via the shared [FieldGridColumns.cs](src/EmberTern.App/Views/FieldGridColumns.cs)) merge the separate **Domain** + **TYPE OF** columns into ONE "Domain / Column" cell — a two-tab `SearchableComboBox`: **Domain** (the rich domain list) + **Column** (a `TableColumnPicker` → commits a `ColumnRef` → the field's `TypeOf` = `COLUMN T.C` → DDL emits `TYPE OF COLUMN T.C`). The Table Detail + New Table grids are **NOT** touched here — that's Krok 3 (the riskiest step, kept separate). **1500 → 1508 tests** (+8). Build 0/0.

**Row VM** ([ProcedureFieldRowBase.cs](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/ProcedureFieldRowBase.cs)) — new unified `SelectedTypeSource` (object, bound TwoWay to the picker's `SelectedItem`): `DomainSpec`→`DomainName`, `ColumnRef`→`TypeOf`, `null`→clear both (Domain/TYPE OF are mutually exclusive — the existing `OnDomainNameChanged`/`OnTypeOfChanged` hooks clear the other). Getter returns a non-null value whenever either is set (resolved `DomainSpec`, parsed `ColumnRef`, or a string fallback for a `TYPE OF <domain>` form) so the picker's ✕ clear stays visible. New `TypeSourceDisplay` (string, bound to `SelectionBoxText`) = domain name | `TABLE.COLUMN` ("COLUMN " stripped) | empty. `[NotifyPropertyChangedFor]` on `_domainName`/`_typeOf` keeps both reactive; `AvailableDomains.CollectionChanged` also re-raises `SelectedTypeSource` so a domain-typed row resolves once domains load. `ComposeType`/`TypeText` unchanged — TYPE OF round-trips losslessly (`LoadType` already parses `TYPE OF …`).

**The load-bearing constraint (your handoff's "świadoma decyzja architektoniczna"):** a `SearchableComboBoxSection` is a plain `AvaloniaObject` and the `TableColumnPicker` is parented only into the top-level popup — **neither is in the visual tree**, so DataContext-inheritance bindings don't reach them. The merged cell's `FuncDataTemplate` therefore **binds only the `SearchableComboBox` itself** (`SelectedItem`/`SelectionBoxText`, which IS in the tree) and **sets the section/picker data imperatively from the row VM** (`domainSection.ItemsSource`, `tablePicker.Tables`, `tablePicker.ColumnsLoader`) in a `Populate()` run on the cell's `DataContextChanged` (re-runs on DataGrid row recycling). Do NOT try to bind the section via DataContext.

**Wiring** — `IFieldRowOwner` + both owner VMs ([ProcedureDetailTabViewModel](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/ProcedureDetailTabViewModel.cs), [TriggerDetailTabViewModel](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/TriggerDetailTabViewModel.cs)) gained `AvailableTables` + `IColumnsLoader? ColumnsLoader`; the row forwards both from its owner. New [DelegateColumnsLoader.cs](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/DelegateColumnsLoader.cs) adapts `MainWindowViewModel.EnsureColumnsAsync` (cached, lazy per-table) to `IColumnsLoader`. All four construction sites (`CreateProcedureDetail`/`CreateTriggerDetail` + the `NewProcedure`/`NewTrigger` flows) now set `ColumnsLoader` and load the table list (`LoadProcedureListsAsync` extended to also list tables; trigger already did). Strings: `FieldTypeSourceHeader` = "Domain / Column", `FieldTypeSourceDomainTab` = "Domain", `FieldTypeSourceColumnTab` = "Column" (English, matching the grid's other headers — the spec's "Domena/Kolumna" was the author's working-language description).

**Tests** ([Faza4MergedTypeSourceTests.cs](tests/EmberTern.Tests/Faza4MergedTypeSourceTests.cs), +8): pick domain → DomainName + display + canonical type; pick column → `COLUMN T.C` + `TYPE OF COLUMN T.C` + display strips "COLUMN "; clear drops both; switch domain↔column clears the other; load `TYPE OF COLUMN T.C` round-trips (display + `ColumnRef` getter + verbatim `TypeText`); plain base type has no type-source; `AvailableTables`/`ColumnsLoader` forwarded from owner. The picker's filtering/popup/two-pane UX is inherently visual + DB-driven → user live-smoke (Krok 2).

**Remaining: Krok 3** — apply the same merged Domain/Column cell to the **Table Detail (Pola)** + **New Table** grids (separate `FieldRowViewModel`/`NewTableFieldRowViewModel`, their own type system — not `ProcedureFieldRowBase`). Historically the most regression-prone area → its own commit + its own live smoke, per the staged plan.

### SearchableComboBox — crash + flicker fixes (shipped 2026-06-28)

Two live-DB bugs in the merged "Domena/Kolumna" picker (Krok 3), both the same root cause surfaced differently. Found with a headless probe that built the real merged column + DataGrid + binding and drove a domain change (the probe was kept only long enough to capture the stack, then removed as flaky — `Dispatcher.Post` + headless `session.Dispatch` hangs, gotcha #94 territory).

- **Hard crash on setting a domain/table** (`ae1ad0c`): the popup's `TabControl.SelectionChanged` handler re-applied the filter, which set a `ListBox.ItemsSource` **synchronously inside the selection-model's batch update** → `InvalidOperationException: Cannot change source while update is in progress`, thrown from a routed-event handler → unhandled → the process died with no dialog ("app just closes"). Same hazard found + fixed in `TableColumnPicker.OnTableSelected` (table-list selection set the column-list `ItemsSource` synchronously) — deferred the column load via `Dispatcher.UIThread.Post`. Also moved `TableColumnPicker`'s `Tables`-change reaction off an `AddClassHandler` registered in the **instance** ctor (a new global class handler per realized grid cell — leak + O(n²) refilters) to an `OnPropertyChanged` override.
- **Flicker/loop after deferring** (`90278f2`): `SelectionChanged` BUBBLES. The `tabs.SelectionChanged` handler was also catching the inner domain-`ListBox`'s SelectionChanged bubbling up → re-applied the filter → reset the ListBox source → re-fired its SelectionChanged → bubbled back → ∞ (flickering row highlight). The `Dispatcher.Post` had merely converted the synchronous crash into an async loop. Final fix: the tabs handler reacts **only to the TabControl's own tab switch** (`ReferenceEquals(e.Source, tabs)`), ignoring bubbling child-list selections; the tab-switch re-filter stays deferred via `Dispatcher.Post` to avoid the in-update crash.

Also added a global fatal-crash logger in [Program.cs](src/EmberTern.App/Program.cs) (`AppDomain.UnhandledException` / `UnobservedTaskException` / startup → `%TEMP%/EmberTern-debug.log` `FATAL` line) so a future silent UI-thread crash leaves a stack. The `[Binding] ... 'Value is null'` lines for `ActiveViewDetail`/`ActiveProcedureDetail`/`ActiveNewTable`/`ActiveTableDetail` toolbar buttons are harmless inactive-tab noise (those `Active*` are null when their tab isn't selected), not errors.

Build 0/0, 1524 tests green (the flaky probe was reverted), app smoke-launches clean. User confirmed the live domain-set on a table works (no crash, no flicker).

### SearchableComboBox — Faza 3 (domain-derived display + stale-Size fix) (shipped 2026-06-20)

Faza 2 verified on the live DB (rich domain list good). Then Faza 3 — after picking a domain the field's cells show the domain's resolved definition, with full parity across the editors and no DDL regression:

- **Shared `FieldTypeRules`** ([FieldTypeRules.cs](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/FieldTypeRules.cs)) — `UsesSize`/`UsesScale`/`UsesSubType(baseType)`. Fixes the user-reported **stale-Size bug**: changing a type away from a size-bearing one (VARCHAR→SMALLINT) now clears the irrelevant Size/Scale/SubType cells. Applied in `ProcedureFieldRowBase.OnBaseTypeChanged`, `FieldRowViewModel.OnTypeTextChanged`, `NewTableFieldRowViewModel.OnTypeChanged`.
- **Procedure/Trigger** (`ProcedureFieldRowBase.SyncTypeDisplayFromDomain`): extended from Type/Size/Scale to also mirror **SubType + Charset + NotNull** from the (now-enriched) `DomainSpec`. Stays display-only — `ComposeType` returns the domain name, so the DDL is unaffected (gotcha: domain wins).
- **Table Detail** (`FieldRowViewModel`): NEW domain→Type/Size/Scale display mirror on `OnDomainNameChanged` (the real gap — it previously kept showing the old type). **DDL-safe**: `EnqueueRowEdits` builds the ALTER from `DomainName` when a domain is set (`typeClause = row.DomainName`), so mirroring `TypeText`/`Size`/`Scale` for display never changes the generated statement. Existing domain columns already load correct (`TypeText = original.Type` = resolved), so the mirror only fires on an interactive pick.
- **New Table**: already shows the resolved type via `EffectiveTypeDisplay` (the Type column) — only the stale-Size fix was needed.
- **Add Field dialog path** (follow-up): a field added to an existing table via the "Add Field" dialog with a domain showed a blank Type cell — `TableDetailTabViewModel.BuildDisplayFieldInfo` used `FormatTypeOrDomain(def)` which returns the DOMAIN NAME (not a base type the grid can render). Fixed: it now resolves the domain's type from `AvailableDomains` for the display row (the Domain column still drives the DDL), giving the dialog-add path the same parity as inline grid edits.

The control's header is an `IDataTemplate HeaderTemplate` (built per popup — a shared Control can't be parented into multiple popups); `HeaderContent` (object) replaced. Popup `MinWidth` raised to 480 for the 6 columns. All four Domain pickers (FieldGridColumns / NewTable / TableDetail / AddFieldDialog) use the shared row + header templates → identical rich list everywhere.

TableDetail) **+ Faza 4** (Table-column tab = TYPE OF COLUMN + merge Domain/TYPE OF into one "Domena/Kolumna" column). Filtering/popup/visual are the user's live smoke.

### Searchable in-grid pickers + Type/Domain disable + body blank-line (shipped 2026-06-20)

Four post-live-test fixes on the Trigger/Procedure/Table field grids. **1467 → 1472 tests** (+5). Build clean (0/0), 8s smoke OK. Still on `feat/trigger-editor` (not merged). DB/visual confirmation (popup, filtering) is the user's live smoke check — a dropdown can't be opened from a headless build.

**`SearchablePicker` is now the app-wide standard for in-cell domain/type lookups (P1).** The shared `FieldGridColumns` (Procedure Input/Output/Variables + Trigger Variables) and the two table grids (`NewTableTabView`, `TableDetailTabView` Pola) built their Type/Domain cells as a plain `ComboBox` with `IsTextSearchEnabled` (type-ahead **jump** only — looked like a normal combo, no filtering). All swapped to `SearchablePicker` (the `AutoCompleteBox` subclass: `FilterMode=Contains`, `MinimumPrefixLength=0`, show-all-on-focus) so the user can type-to-**filter** a 2000-row domain/table list anywhere — form, dialog, grid, editor. This reverses the prior milestone's "keep ComboBox in grids (risky)" decision at the user's explicit request ("SearchablePicker powinien być standardem aplikacji"). The previously-flagged risk (AutoCompleteBox `SelectedItem`→null on partial-typed text) is handled by null-guarded wrappers: `SelectedDomainSpec` (object, already ignored null) + a new `SelectedTypeItem` (string) on `ProcedureFieldRowBase` and `NewTableFieldRowViewModel` whose setter ignores null so a mid-filter keystroke can't clear the type. Object columns get `ValueMemberBinding="{ReflectionBinding Name}"` + an `ItemTemplate`; string (Type) columns need neither.

**Popup-under-editor (P2)** — treated as a critical UX bug. `Program.cs` uses `UsePlatformDetect()` with no `OverlayPopups` override, so on Windows Avalonia popups are separate top-level `PopupRoot` windows that float above the AvaloniaEdit body editor; the `SearchablePicker` dropdown (`MaxDropDownHeight=320`, auto-flip placement) replaces the ComboBox popup. The likely cause was the ComboBox in-cell dropdown's placement/height in the bounded grid region adjacent to the custom-rendered editor. **Needs the user's live confirmation** — if clipping persists, the next step is explicit popup placement/host config (can't reproduce headless).

**Type/Domain disable, not empty columns (P4).** The Procedure/Trigger grids had **no** gating — a domain-typed variable showed a stale `Type=INTEGER` next to `Domain=T_ID`. Added visible-but-disabled gates to `ProcedureFieldRowBase` (`IsTypeEnabled`/`IsTypeOfEnabled`/`IsSizeEnabled`/`IsScaleEnabled`/`IsSubTypeEnabled`/`IsCharsetEnabled`, driven by `HasDomain`/`HasTypeOf`, with `[NotifyPropertyChangedFor]` on `DomainName`/`TypeOf`); the type-construction cells (TYPE OF/Size/Scale/Sub Type/Charset) became per-row disable-able template cells in `FieldGridColumns` (a `DataGridTextColumn` can't bind per-row `IsEnabled` — gotcha #83/#124), and the existing `SyncTypeDisplayFromDomain` already mirrors the domain's resolved type into the disabled cells so they show the effective type rather than going blank — matching the table editor. NewTable/TableDetail Pola already had their own gates (kept).

**No blank line before BEGIN (P3).** `DdlGenerator.BuildProcedureBody` dropped the `if (sb.Length > 0) sb.Append('\n')` separator, so the DECLARE section flows directly into `BEGIN` (`DECLARE … ID_NAGL T_ID;\nBEGIN`, not `…;\n\nBEGIN`). Shared by Procedure + Trigger.

**Trigger↔Procedure parity audit** (done as part of this task): no gaps. Easy↔Source roundtrip, Compile, Format(Alt+F+toolbar), Comment/Uncomment, IntelliSense (+`NEW.`/`OLD.` on Trigger), dirty/WorkGuard, persistence, auto-naming, table-change, Variable grid, unified 5-section toolbar (`ActiveCollection()` routes Trigger Variables), keyboard shortcuts — all present. Intentional absences (correct): no Execute / Result tab / editable Cursors+Subprograms grids (triggers aren't executed; cursors/subprograms preserved verbatim).

Two commits (`418cbfa` Procedure/Trigger + shared; `10b14f4` NewTable + TableDetail Pola). Tests: +5 (blank-line pin, 4 gate/`SelectedTypeItem`-null-guard).

The Table designer's "discard pending changes" button (next to ⚡ Compile) now has a counterpart on the **View / Procedure / Trigger** source editors, and **all four discard/revert actions now confirm** before throwing away uncompiled work (the user's explicit ask: an accidental click must not lose edits). **1508 → 1520 tests** (+12). Build clean (0/0).

**Two flavours, one contract** (recorded in the editor contract above + gotcha #143):
- **Table designer** (buffered model): `DiscardPendingChangesCommand` clears the in-memory `PendingChanges` and reprojects the grids to catalog truth — no DB round-trip. Gated `CanDiscardPending => HasPendingChanges`. Converted from a synchronous `void` to `async Task` so it can `await RequestConfirmAsync(...)` first; XAML binding (`DiscardPendingChangesCommand`) and the `NotifyCanExecuteChanged` call site are unchanged.
- **Source editors** ([ViewDetailTabViewModel](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/ViewDetailTabViewModel.cs) / [ProcedureDetailTabViewModel](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/ProcedureDetailTabViewModel.cs) / [TriggerDetailTabViewModel](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/TriggerDetailTabViewModel.cs)): new `RevertChangesCommand` = reload-from-DB via the existing `RefreshAsync()` (the source-editor analog of catalog-truth). Gated `CanRevertChanges => IsDirty && !IsNew` — a not-yet-created object has no DB state to revert to, so the button **disables in the New flow** (use Close, which already routes through the WorkGuard). Each VM gained a `ConfirmationRequested` event + `RequestConfirmAsync` helper (same shape as TableDetail; default-true when no handler so tests proceed), wired to `MainWindowViewModel.RequestConfirmAsync` in `CreateViewDetail`/`CreateProcedureDetail`/`CreateTriggerDetail`.

**Dirty→button-enabled sync.** The three source VMs tracked dirty with a plain field + getter (`IsDirty`) that never raised `PropertyChanged`. Centralized `MarkDirty`/`ClearDirty` through a new `SetDirty(bool)` that, on a transition, raises `OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsDirty))` + `RevertChangesCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged()` so the toolbar button greys/ungreys as edits appear/clear (mirrors the table's `OnPendingChangesCollectionChanged` notify).

**Confirmation copy** ([UiStrings.cs](src/EmberTern.App/UiStrings.cs)): `RevertChangesTooltip` / `RevertChangesConfirm{Title,Format,Yes}` (source editors, "reload from the database") and `FieldEditDiscardConfirm{Title,Format,Yes}` (table, "pending structural changes"). All destructive (`IsDestructive = true`), object name interpolated into the message.

**XAML** ([MainWindow.axaml](src/EmberTern.App/Views/MainWindow.axaml)): an `Icon.Undo` `Button.icon` after each View/Procedure/Trigger Compile button, gated on the matching `IsViewDetailTabActive`/`IsProcedureDetailTabActive`/`IsTriggerDetailTabActive` and bound to `Active*.RevertChangesCommand`. The table's existing discard button is unchanged.

**Tests** ([RevertChangesTests.cs](tests/EmberTern.Tests/RevertChangesTests.cs), +12): `CanRevertChanges` gating (clean=false, dirty+existing=true, new-even-when-dirty=false) for View/Procedure/Trigger; revert raises a destructive, object-named `ConfirmRequest`; no-handler revert doesn't throw; Table discard cancel **keeps** `PendingChanges`, confirm **clears** them. The existing `TableDesignerBufferedTests.Discard_RevertsWorkingModelToCatalog` updated to `await DiscardPendingChangesCommand.ExecuteAsync(null)`. The source-editor reload effect needs a live FB (RefreshAsync is a no-op with null readers), so those tests pin the gate + confirmation contract, not the reload — consistent with the codebase's DB-path test precedent.

**Gotcha — promote to architecture lore.**

143. **Every object editor must ship a Revert/Discard button beside Compile, and it must confirm.** Two correct shapes: a *buffered* designer (Table) discards its in-memory pending model + reprojects to catalog truth (no DB hit); a *source* editor (View/Procedure/Trigger/future Function/Package) reloads from the DB via `RefreshAsync` and is gated `IsDirty && !IsNew` (no DB state exists for a not-yet-created object — disable, don't no-op). The confirmation is non-negotiable (an accidental click loses uncompiled work): raise the VM's `ConfirmationRequested` (destructive `ConfirmRequest`, default-true with no handler for tests) and wire it in the editor's factory. A plain-field `IsDirty` won't drive the button's enabled state — route dirty transitions through a `SetDirty` that raises `PropertyChanged` + `RevertChangesCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged()`. When adding the next editor: command + `ConfirmationRequested` event + factory wiring + one `Icon.Undo` toolbar button gated on the editor's `IsXxxTabActive`.

144. **`SelectionChanged` BUBBLES — a `TabControl.SelectionChanged` handler also fires for every inner ItemsControl's selection bubbling up; never react to it unguarded, and never change an `ItemsSource` synchronously inside a selection-model update.** In the popup of a tabbed picker (`SearchableComboBox` with a `TabControl` over per-tab `ListBox`es), `tabs.SelectionChanged` caught the inner domain-`ListBox`'s SelectionChanged; the handler re-applied the filter (set the ListBox `ItemsSource`), which (a) synchronously threw `InvalidOperationException: Cannot change source while update is in progress` from a routed-event handler → unhandled → silent process death, and (b) once deferred with `Dispatcher.Post`, formed an ∞ bubbling loop (set source → re-fire ListBox SelectionChanged → bubble → re-post → …, seen as a flickering highlight). Two rules: **(1)** guard a container's routed-event handler to the container itself — `if (ReferenceEquals(e.Source, theTabControl))` — so bubbling child events are ignored; **(2)** any `ItemsSource` mutation triggered from a selection-changed path must be deferred via `Dispatcher.UIThread.Post` (it's illegal during the selection-model's batch update). The same `ItemsSource`-during-selection hazard applies to a plain `ListBox.SelectionChanged` that rebuilds another list (`TableColumnPicker.OnTableSelected` → defer the column load). Headless can't reliably test the Post path (gotcha #94) — verify by live smoke; a global fatal-crash logger in `Program.cs` (`%TEMP%/EmberTern-debug.log` `FATAL`) captures any future silent UI-thread crash.

145. **A function editor has TWO distinct "Result" concepts — never merge them.** Easy-mode **Result** = the function's return-TYPE metadata: a single, unnamed `RETURNS <type>` row (+ a `Deterministic` flag) that the user edits and that drives the generated DDL. The **Execute Result** tab = the runtime OUTPUT of running the function (`SELECT fn(…) FROM RDB$DATABASE` on the Data lane). They share no state and no control: the Easy Result sub-tab is a one-row field grid (no Add/Remove/Move — `IsEasyCollectionEditable` is false there so the toolbar's collection section hides), while the Execute Result tab is a dynamic-column read-only grid fed by `ExecResult` + the persistent exec-info bar. Procedure's single top-level "Result" tab (execution output) does NOT map 1:1 onto a function — for a function the execution-output tab is renamed **"Execute Result"** so the word "Result" is free for the return-type metadata. Keep the two strictly separate in the VM, the view, and the labels.

### Function Editor + SourceObjectDetailTabViewModel base (Etaps 0–6, shipped 2026-06-29)

PSQL functions now open in a dedicated **Function Editor** mirroring the Procedure editor ("Function = Procedure + the differences from a function's nature"), built on a newly-extracted shared base. **COMPLETE — merged to `master` (2026-06-29).** 1524 → **1583 tests**, build 0/0, smoke clean. Etap 6 (live-FB smoke: connect → open function → Source/Easy → edit → Compile → Execute → New Function → restart) is the user's manual pass against a real Firebird, per the codebase's DB-path precedent.

**Etap 0 — `SourceObjectDetailTabViewModel` (rule-of-three base).** Procedure + Trigger were duplicating a large VM skeleton; extracted it into an abstract base shared by the PSQL-routine editors (Procedure, Trigger, Function): dirty tracking + `IUnsavedWorkSource`, Source⇄Easy toggle skeleton, Format/Comment/Uncomment, the editable Variables grid + generic row helpers, Dependencies trees, editable Description (`COMMENT ON …`), Compile + Revert + load/refresh lifecycle, `IFieldRowOwner` (domain/type/table-column pickers). Each editor supplies only its essence through ~10 small hooks (`BuildFullSource`, `TryApplySource`, `LoadCoreAsync`, `CommentSql`, `TryParseName`, `ValidateBeforeCompile`, the message strings). Procedure + Trigger refactored onto it with **no behavior change** (their full test suites stayed green before/after). The base came out clean — no awkward overrides — so the user's "abort to sibling if it gets artificial" guard was never triggered. View Detail intentionally stays OFF this base (a view has no PSQL body/variables/params — a different family). The named `ProcedureCreated`/`TriggerCreated` events collapsed into the base's single `ObjectCreated`.

**Etap 1 — Core.** [FunctionSignatureParser](src/EmberTern.Core/Sql/FunctionSignatureParser.cs) parses `CREATE [OR ALTER] FUNCTION name [(args)] RETURNS <type> [DETERMINISTIC] AS body` (args reuse `ProcedureSignatureParser.ParseSegment`; the single return type — incl. `VARCHAR(n)` / domain / `TYPE OF COLUMN` / `CHARACTER SET` / `COLLATE` — is read verbatim to the top-level `AS`, with a trailing `[NOT] DETERMINISTIC` split off). [DdlGenerator.BuildCreateOrAlterFunction](src/EmberTern.Core/Metadata/DdlGenerator.cs) (a single `RETURNS <type>` line, not a param block; reuses `AppendProcedureParamLines` for args) + `BuildCommentFunction` (`COMMENT ON FUNCTION`).

**Etap 2 — Firebird readers.** [FirebirdDdlReader](src/EmberTern.Firebird/FirebirdDdlReader.cs): `FetchFunctionSourceAsync` (editable `CREATE OR ALTER FUNCTION`) + `FetchFunctionBodyAsync` (`RDB$FUNCTION_SOURCE` — body alone, like procedures, gotcha #114); the stub `BuildFunctionDdlAsync` rebuilt into a real reconstruction — `RDB$FUNCTIONS.RDB$RETURN_ARGUMENT` splits the return value from the input args (`RDB$FUNCTION_ARGUMENTS ⋈ RDB$FIELDS`), `RDB$DETERMINISTIC_FLAG` gates the keyword. [FirebirdTableDetailReader](src/EmberTern.Firebird/FirebirdTableDetailReader.cs): `GetFunctionSignatureAsync` (args + formatted return type + deterministic), `GetFunctionDescriptionAsync`, `GetFunctionDependenciesAsync` (RDB$DEPENDENCIES type **15**). `FunctionSignatureInfo` (Core) — function args reuse `ProcedureParameterInfo`. **Known limitation (mirrors procedures):** a domain-typed argument/return shows its resolved underlying type in the loaded DDL/source, not the domain name.

**Etap 3 — VM.** [FunctionDetailTabViewModel](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/FunctionDetailTabViewModel.cs) on the base: Input params → **Arguments**; Output params → **Result** (a function returns ONE value → a single row, no Add/Remove/Move, + a `Deterministic` flag; `IsEasyCollectionEditable` is false on the Result sub-tab so the toolbar's collection section hides there); Variables/Cursors/Subprograms kept (Firebird allows them); **Execute** builds `SELECT fn(@p0,…) FROM RDB$DATABASE` on the Data lane (reuses `ExecuteProcedureDialog` + the procedure exec/outcome shape) → runtime output goes to a separate **Execute Result** tab, distinct from the Easy-mode Result metadata (the user's explicit Result vs. Execute-Result split). `ProcedureParamRowViewModel` ctor/`From` widened to `IFieldRowOwner` so Function reuses the param/argument row VM.

**Etap 4 — View.** [FunctionDetailTabView](src/EmberTern.App/Views/FunctionDetailTabView.axaml)(.cs) — a trimmed mirror of the Procedure view (Editor Source/Easy with Arguments/Result/Variables/Cursors/Subprograms sub-tabs; Description/Dependencies/DDL/Execute Result). `FieldGridColumns.Build` gained an `includeName` flag (default true) for the unnamed return row.

**Etap 5 — routing/persistence/toolbar.** `OpensAsFunctionDetail` + `CreateFunctionDetail` + `RunFunctionExecuteAsync` (Data lane) + `OnOpenDdlRequested` branch + `(Kind,Name)` dedup; `NewFunction` command + `OnFunctionCreated`; `IsFunctionDetailTabActive`/`ActiveFunctionDetail` + notify chain + SelectTab lazy-load + tracked-collection child; unified 5-section toolbar buttons (mode/Compile/Revert/Execute/Format/Comment/Uncomment/Commit/Rollback) gated on `IsFunctionDetailTabActive`; native `WorkspaceTabKind.FunctionDetail` (VM + Core) persistence (Snapshot/Restore) + global `FunctionEasyMode` hybrid preference; New Function toolbar button + composed `Icon.FunctionPlus`.

**Execute (Data lane) shares with Procedure (rule-of-TWO, deliberately duplicated, not extracted):** the exec-result/paging/exec-info block is duplicated between Procedure and Function VMs rather than pushed into the base — Trigger doesn't execute, so it's a 2-consumer block; extracting an intermediate "executable routine" base for two consumers was judged worse for readability (per the user's "czytelność > DRY"). A future 3rd executable editor would justify extraction.

**UDF (FB ≤2.5 / external) — deliberately minimal (per the user's "na razie nie inwestuj czasu").** A legacy UDF has no PSQL source: `FetchFunctionSourceAsync` returns a comment/placeholder, Easy-mode parse fails gracefully (stays in Source with `FunctionParseFailedNotice`), and compiling a comment fails with a clear server error. No special read-only gating was built — full UDF support is a separate future topic.

### Generator Detail — dedicated 3-tab surface (shipped 2026-06-29)

Generators (sequences) open in a dedicated **Generator Detail** experience instead of a plain DDL tab: three tabs — **Generator** (the editable form: Name / Current value / Initial value / Increment / Description), **Dependencies** (the dual dependency tree), **DDL** (read-only reconstructed `CREATE SEQUENCE`). **1583 → 1626 tests** (+43). Build clean (0/0), 12s smoke OK.

**Architecture decision — a focused form-based `GeneratorDetailTabViewModel`, NOT a `SourceObjectDetailTabViewModel` subclass.** A generator has no PSQL body, no Source/Easy mode, no variables/params, no fields, no data preview — so the source-object base (Procedure/Trigger/Function) doesn't fit and would be all-overrides. It's modeled on `ViewDetailTabViewModel` (dirty tracking + `IUnsavedWorkSource` + `ConfirmationRequested` + `EnsureLoadedAsync`/`RefreshAsync` + `OpenObjectRequested` + an `ObjectCreated`-style event) but stripped to a small form. **Reuse is at the reader / DDL-generator / dependency-tree level, not via inheritance** — same principle as View (gotcha #112) and Procedure (gotcha #113).

**Reuse (directly):** `FirebirdDdlReader.FetchDdlAsync(Generator)` (already reconstructed `CREATE SEQUENCE`) for the DDL tab; `TableDetailTabViewModel.BuildDependencyTree` + `MapObjectTypeToKind` + `DependencyInfo` + the dual dependency-tree templates + `OpenObjectRequested` routing (a generator dependency double-click opens the referencing trigger/procedure); the `DdlGenerator` quoting + `BuildRelationComment` helper (→ `COMMENT ON SEQUENCE`); the View Detail dirty/Revert/WorkGuard/persistence/routing/toolbar patterns; the `Icon.Generator` glyph + `IconColor_Generator` (no new color token).

**Core** — [GeneratorInfo.cs](src/EmberTern.Core/Metadata/GeneratorInfo.cs) (init-only POCO: Name / CurrentValue / InitialValue / Increment / Description). [DdlGenerator.cs](src/EmberTern.Core/Metadata/DdlGenerator.cs): `BuildCreateSequence(name, start, increment)` (emits `START WITH` only for non-zero start, `INCREMENT BY` only for non-1 increment → a plain `CREATE SEQUENCE "N"` stays FB3-portable), `BuildDropSequence`, `BuildAlterSequenceRestart` (current value, FB3+), `BuildAlterSequenceStartWith` / `BuildAlterSequenceIncrement` (FB4+), `BuildCommentSequence` (→ `BuildRelationComment("SEQUENCE", …)`). `WorkspaceTabKind.GeneratorDetail` added to the Core persistence enum.

**Firebird** ([FirebirdTableDetailReader.cs](src/EmberTern.Firebird/FirebirdTableDetailReader.cs)) — `GetGeneratorInfoAsync` (current value via `GEN_ID(name, 0)`, best-effort; initial/increment from `RDB$GENERATORS` on FB3+, defaulting 0/1 below; description). `GetGeneratorDependenciesAsync` + `GeneratorDependsOnSql`/`GeneratorDependedOnBySql` (`RDB$DEPENDENCIES` type **14** = Generator). Metadata-lane access (`MetaConnection`/`MetaLock`/`MetaTx`), lock captured once (gotcha #98). **FB-version gate** (`FirebirdDdlReader.ParseServerMajor`) avoids a doomed "column not found" query on FB 2.5 — gotcha #146.

**App** — [GeneratorDetailTabViewModel.cs](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/GeneratorDetailTabViewModel.cs) (numeric form fields are `decimal?` bound to `NumericUpDown`, converted to `long` for DDL — gotcha #51's NumericUpDown bridge; UPPERCASE name coercion in the New flow per gotcha #141; baseline values captured on load so Save emits the minimum-set of statements). `SaveCommand` builds the diff DDL (`BuildSaveSql`, pure + internal) and runs it through `FirebirdDdlExecutor` — **autonomous auto-commit (Phase A), so no working transaction and no Commit/Rollback pair on a generator tab** (like Trigger; excluded from `ShowTransactionButtons`). `RevertChangesCommand` (gated `IsDirty && !IsNew`, confirms) reloads from DB; `DeleteCommand` (gated `!IsNew`, confirms) raises `DeleteRequested` → owner runs `DROP SEQUENCE` + closes the tab + refreshes the tree. [GeneratorDetailTabView.axaml](src/EmberTern.App/Views/GeneratorDetailTabView.axaml)(.cs) — the form + dual dependency trees + read-only DDL editor (theme swap, no autocomplete). [WorkspaceTabViewModel.cs](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/WorkspaceTabViewModel.cs) — `GeneratorDetail` enum value + `CreateGeneratorDetail` factory + property + `UnsavedWork` case.

**Routing/persistence/toolbar** ([MainWindowViewModel.cs](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/MainWindowViewModel.cs), [MainWindow.axaml](src/EmberTern.App/Views/MainWindow.axaml)) — strictly parallel to the View path: `OpensAsGeneratorDetail` + `CreateGeneratorDetail` factory (wires `OpenObjectRequested`/`ConfirmationRequested`/`DeleteRequested`) + `OnOpenDdlRequested` branch + `(Kind,Name)` dedup; `NewGenerator` command (`Icon.GeneratorPlus`, in the New-X toolbar group) + `OnGeneratorCreated` (refresh tree → close New tab → reopen real generator); `OnGeneratorDeleteRequested`; `IsGeneratorDetailTabActive`/`ActiveGeneratorDetail` + notify chain + `SelectTab` lazy-load + `IsClosableTabActive`; native `CoreTabKind.GeneratorDetail` persistence (Snapshot skips transient New tabs / Restore lazy-loads); `CanCreateGenerator` re-notify on connection change; Save/Revert/Delete editor-toolbar buttons gated on `IsGeneratorDetailTabActive`; sibling `<views:GeneratorDetailTabView>`. New strings in [UiStrings.cs](src/EmberTern.App/UiStrings.cs); composed `Icon.GeneratorPlus` (hash + bottom-right `+`) in [IconGeometries.axaml](src/EmberTern.App/Themes/IconGeometries.axaml).

**Double-click in the metadata tree** opens Generator Detail (not a DDL tab) — identical to Tables/Views/Procedures/Triggers, via the shared `OpensAsGeneratorDetail` predicate in `OnOpenDdlRequested`. Dependency navigation reuses `MapObjectTypeToKind` (which already maps "Generator").

**Tests** ([GeneratorDetailTests.cs](tests/EmberTern.Tests/GeneratorDetailTests.cs), +43): `OpensAsGeneratorDetail` predicate; factory (Save/Delete capability); direct-open → GeneratorDetail tab (not DDL) + dedup; native restore; capture persists as GeneratorDetail; New command re-notifies on connect; DDL-generator shape (CREATE/ALTER RESTART/START WITH/INCREMENT BY/DROP/COMMENT + empty-name throw); `BuildSaveSql` (New → CREATE [+COMMENT/+RESTART]; existing no-change → empty; existing current-change → RESTART); dirty tracking + Save/Delete/Revert gating; `DeleteCommand` raises `DeleteRequested` after confirm; `GetUnsavedWork` (clean/new/existing); dependency navigation; reader SQL type-14 shape + `MapObjectType(14)`.

**Known limitations / deferred.** Current-value change emits `ALTER SEQUENCE … RESTART WITH` (FB3+); initial-value / increment changes emit FB4+ `ALTER SEQUENCE … START WITH / INCREMENT BY` — on FB 2.5 those degrade (FB 2.5 only has `SET GENERATOR`/no increment), surfaced as a server error on Save. On FB3 the initial/increment fields show 0/1 if the catalog columns aren't read (version-gated). Live-DB smoke (open generator → edit current value → Save → New Generator → Delete) against the user's FB3/FB5 is the user's manual pass, per the codebase's DB-path precedent.

**Gotcha — promote to architecture lore.**

146. **`RDB$GENERATORS.RDB$INITIAL_VALUE` / `RDB$GENERATOR_INCREMENT` don't exist on Firebird 2.5 — version-gate the SELECT, don't issue it and catch.** Those columns were added in FB3 (empirically confirmed present on FB3 and FB5). Issuing a SELECT that references them on FB 2.5 throws `FbException` ("column not found"), and a failed command mid-transaction can mark an active working tx for rollback (readers attach to the working tx when one is open). So gate on `FirebirdDdlReader.ParseServerMajor(connection.ServerVersion) >= 3`: read the columns on FB3+, default initial=0 / increment=1 below. The current value (`GEN_ID(name, 0)`) is version-independent (best-effort try/catch for system sequences). Same "don't run a doomed catalog query" discipline as the FB-version gates for procedure/function source.

### Generator Detail — bugfix sprint (semantics, shipped 2026-06-29)

Five real-DB bugs in the just-shipped Generator Detail, **verified empirically against local FB3 + FB5 databases via `isql`** (no guessing — the prior implementation conflated a sequence's *definition* with its *runtime counter*). **1626 → 1635 tests.** Build 0/0, smoke OK.

**Empirically verified Firebird semantics** (the truth the fix is built on):

| Operation | FB3 `GEN_ID(,0)` | FB5 `GEN_ID(,0)` |
|---|---|---|
| `CREATE SEQUENCE G` | 0 (first `NEXT`=1) | 0 (first `NEXT`=1) |
| `CREATE SEQUENCE G START WITH 10000` | 10000 (first `NEXT`=10001) | **9999** (first `NEXT`=10000) |
| `ALTER SEQUENCE G RESTART WITH v` | v | **v−1** (version-DEPENDENT) |
| `SET GENERATOR G TO v` | v | v (version-INDEPENDENT) |
| `RDB$INITIAL_VALUE` after `START WITH 10000` | 10000 | 10000 |

So: FB4+ adopted SQL-standard `START WITH`/`RESTART WITH` (the stored counter is `logical − increment`, so the first generated value equals the typed number); FB3 stores the counter = the typed number (first generated value is `+increment`). `SET GENERATOR … TO` sets the raw counter directly and behaves identically on both.

**Root cause of Problems 1/2/3 (one bug).** The New-generator `BuildSaveSql` appended `ALTER SEQUENCE … RESTART WITH <CurrentValue>` after the `CREATE … START WITH <InitialValue>`. With the default `CurrentValue = 0`, that emitted `RESTART WITH 0`, which on FB5 set the internal counter to `0 − 1 = −1` AND discarded the `START WITH` initial value. Net on FB5: `Current Value = −1`, first generated value `0`, `Initial Value = 10000` silently lost. End-to-end proof (FB5): old SQL → peek `−1` / first `0`; fixed SQL (`CREATE … START WITH 10000` only) → `RDB$INITIAL_VALUE` 10000 / first value 10000.

**Fixes.**
- **New flow emits the DEFINITION only** ([GeneratorDetailTabViewModel.BuildSaveSql](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/GeneratorDetailTabViewModel.cs)): `CREATE SEQUENCE name [START WITH initial] [INCREMENT BY inc]` (+ `COMMENT`). The spurious RESTART is gone. `BuildCreateSequence` still omits `START WITH` for 0 / `INCREMENT BY` for 1, so a default new generator is a plain `CREATE SEQUENCE` → first value 1 (Problem 2). Both `START WITH` and `INCREMENT BY` in `CREATE SEQUENCE` verified working on FB3 + FB5.
- **Current Value edit uses `SET GENERATOR name TO v`** (new `DdlGenerator.BuildSetGenerator`), NOT `RESTART WITH` — version-independent, so `GEN_ID(,0)` becomes exactly the typed value on FB3 and FB5. `BuildAlterSequenceRestart` is kept + shape-pinned (with a doc comment explaining why it's not used for "set current value").
- **De-conflated definition vs runtime (Problem 4)**: Initial Value + Increment are editable only when **creating** (`IsDefinitionEditable => IsNew`); Current Value is editable only on an **existing** generator (`IsCurrentValueEditable => !IsNew`) since it's a consequence of the definition. The View gates the `NumericUpDown`s' `IsEnabled` accordingly. Existing-generator Save therefore emits only `SET GENERATOR` (current) and/or `COMMENT ON SEQUENCE` (description) — never an `ALTER SEQUENCE START WITH/INCREMENT BY`.
- **−1 → 0 display normalization (Problem 3)**: `NormalizeDisplayCurrentValue(raw) => raw < 0 ? 0 : raw` maps Firebird's negative pre-first-use sentinel to 0 for display (and baselines the normalized value so an untouched generator emits no SET on Save). The reader still returns the technically-correct raw value; only the presented value is normalized.
- **Refresh Current Value button (Problem 5)**: `RefreshCurrentValueCommand` + new lightweight reader `FirebirdTableDetailReader.GetGeneratorCurrentValueAsync` (just `GEN_ID(name,0)`). Re-reads ONLY the live counter — no full reload, no dirty flip, no overwrite of Initial/Increment/Description; updates the baseline so the refresh isn't seen as a pending change. Gated `_reader is not null && !IsNew`. For a generator in active ERP use, the user can re-check its value without closing the tab. (`Icon.RefreshCw` button beside the Current Value field.)

**Follow-up — Current Value must equal Initial Value on creation (shipped 2026-06-29).** After the above, the user reported that creating a generator with Initial Value 10000 left **Current Value 9999** (one less) on FB5 — because FB4+ `START WITH n` stores the counter at `n − increment` (the *first generated* value is n, but `GEN_ID(,0)` reads n−1). The user (Firebird-3 ERP dev) wants the classic model where **Current == Initial right after creation** (and the first allocation is Initial + increment), consistently across FB3/FB5. Fix: the New flow now emits **two** statements — `CREATE SEQUENCE name START WITH <initial> [INCREMENT BY <inc>]` (`forceStartWith: true` so `RDB$INITIAL_VALUE` is recorded even for 0) **followed by** `SET GENERATOR name TO <initial>`, which pins the runtime counter to the initial value version-independently. Verified on real FB3 **and** FB5 (incl. one-transaction execution, matching the DDL executor): for initial 10000/0/100-inc5 → `GEN_ID(,0)` = `RDB$INITIAL_VALUE` = 10000/0/100 on both versions, first `NEXT VALUE FOR` = initial+increment. This is NOT the old −1 bug (that appended a RESTART to the *unrelated* default 0, destroying the initial value); here the SET pins to the *same* initial value, intentionally. `BuildCreateSequence` gained an optional `forceStartWith` (default false preserves the portable "plain CREATE for 0" used elsewhere).

**Tests** ([GeneratorDetailTests.cs](tests/EmberTern.Tests/GeneratorDetailTests.cs)): `BuildSetGenerator` shape; New-with-initial emits `START WITH <n>` + `SET GENERATOR … TO <n>` (Current==Initial) and **no** RESTART (the regression pin); New default emits `START WITH 0` + `SET GENERATOR … TO 0`; New-with-increment emits `INCREMENT BY`; `BuildCreateSequence(forceStartWith)` emits `START WITH 0`; existing current-change emits `SET GENERATOR` (not RESTART); `NormalizeDisplayCurrentValue` clamps negatives; `IsDefinitionEditable`/`IsCurrentValueEditable` per new/existing; `CanRefreshCurrentValue` gating. (`RESTART WITH` shape still pinned.) Real-DB behavior (CREATE/SET/RESTART/defaults + Current==Initial after create) verified via isql on FB3 + FB5 — the live app flow against the user's FB3/FB5 is the user's manual confirmation.

**Gotcha — promote to architecture lore.**

147. **Firebird sequence `START WITH` / `RESTART WITH` changed semantics in FB4 — and a sequence has TWO distinct numbers (definition vs runtime). Set the runtime counter with `SET GENERATOR … TO`, never `RESTART WITH`.** FB3: the stored counter equals the typed `START WITH`/`RESTART WITH` value (first generated value is `+increment`). FB4+ (SQL standard): the stored counter is `typed − increment`, so the first *generated* value equals the typed value, but `GEN_ID(,0)` reads `typed − increment` (a fresh `START WITH 10000` reads 9999; `START WITH 0` reads −1). `SET GENERATOR name TO v` sets the raw counter directly to `v` on ALL versions — the only version-independent "make `GEN_ID(,0)` exactly v". The two numbers: **definition** = `START WITH` / `INCREMENT BY` / `RDB$INITIAL_VALUE` (set at CREATE, read-only after — FB3 can't ALTER them) vs **runtime** = `GEN_ID(name,0)` (changeable via `SET GENERATOR`). To make a new generator's runtime Current Value equal its Initial Value across versions (the classic Firebird model the user expects), the New flow emits `CREATE … START WITH <initial>` (forceStartWith, so `RDB$INITIAL_VALUE` is recorded even for 0) **then** `SET GENERATOR … TO <initial>` — pinning the counter to the SAME initial value is correct and intentional. The earlier −1 bug was different: it appended a RESTART/SET to an UNRELATED value (the default 0, not the initial), which destroyed the initial value — never do that. A negative `GEN_ID(,0)` (pre-first-use sentinel) must still be displayed as 0. Verify any sequence-DDL assumption against BOTH FB3 and FB5 with `isql` (incl. single-transaction execution) — they genuinely differ.

### Domain Detail — dedicated 4-tab surface (shipped 2026-06-29)

Domains (user-defined types) open in a dedicated **Domain Detail** experience instead of a plain DDL tab: four tabs — **Domain** (the definition form: Name / Data type / Length / Precision / Scale / Sub type / Character set / Collation / Default value / Check constraint / Not null), **Description** (editable `COMMENT ON DOMAIN`), **Used By** (a SINGLE dependency tree of the table/view columns + PSQL objects that use the domain), **DDL** (read-only reconstructed `CREATE DOMAIN`). **1636 → 1691 tests** (+55). Build clean (0/0), 8s smoke OK.

**Architecture decision — a focused form-based `DomainDetailTabViewModel`, NOT a `SourceObjectDetailTabViewModel` subclass.** A domain has no PSQL body, no Source/Easy mode, no params/variables. It's modeled exactly on `GeneratorDetailTabViewModel` (dirty tracking + `IUnsavedWorkSource` + `ConfirmationRequested` + `EnsureLoadedAsync`/`RefreshAsync` + `OpenObjectRequested` + a `DomainCreated` event + Save/Revert/Delete). **Reuse is at the reader / DDL-generator / dependency-tree level, not via inheritance** — same principle as View (#112), Procedure (#113), Generator.

**Firebird `ALTER DOMAIN` — verified empirically on FB3 + FB5 (`isql`, scratch DBs), per the spec's "don't assume, verify on a real DB":**

| Operation | Supported? |
|---|---|
| `SET DEFAULT` / `DROP DEFAULT` | ✅ both versions |
| `ADD [CONSTRAINT] CHECK (…)` / `DROP CONSTRAINT` (the single check) | ✅ both versions |
| `SET NOT NULL` / `DROP NOT NULL` | ✅ FB3+ |
| `TYPE <newtype>` / `TO <newname>` (rename) | ✅ both versions |
| `COMMENT ON DOMAIN … IS …` | ✅ both versions |
| **change `CHARACTER SET`** | ❌ no syntax → SQL error -104 (Token unknown) |
| **change `COLLATION`** | ❌ no syntax → SQL error -104 |

**EmberTern's editable scope = Default / Check / Description** (per spec). On an existing domain everything else is **read-only**: Name / Data type / Length / Precision / Scale / Sub type / Not null are editable only in the New flow (the `IsDefinitionEditable => IsNew` gate); **Character set + Collation are read-only ALWAYS** — that's a hard Firebird limitation (can never be ALTERed), not just a scope choice. The decision is enforced in the UI (`IsEnabled`/`IsReadOnly` gates) and documented in `DomainInfo` + `DomainDetailTabViewModel`.

**Core** — [DomainInfo.cs](src/EmberTern.Core/Metadata/DomainInfo.cs) (init-only POCO: Name / DataType / Length / Precision / Scale / SubType / CharacterSet / Collation / DefaultValue / CheckConstraint / NotNull / Description). [DdlGenerator.cs](src/EmberTern.Core/Metadata/DdlGenerator.cs): `BuildCreateDomain(DomainInfo)` (readable multi-line `CREATE DOMAIN "N" AS <type> [CHARACTER SET] / DEFAULT / NOT NULL / CHECK / COLLATE`, FB-verified clause order; skips `CHARACTER SET NONE` / `COLLATE NONE`; reuses `NormalizeCheckClause`), `BuildAlterDomainSetDefault` / `BuildAlterDomainDropDefault` / `BuildAlterDomainAddCheck` / `BuildAlterDomainDropConstraint` / `BuildCommentDomain` (→ `BuildRelationComment("DOMAIN", …)`) / `BuildDropDomain` + `ComposeDomainType` private helper. `WorkspaceTabKind.DomainDetail` added to the Core persistence enum.

**Firebird** ([FirebirdTableDetailReader.cs](src/EmberTern.Firebird/FirebirdTableDetailReader.cs)) — `GetDomainInfoAsync(name)` (single-domain read of `RDB$FIELDS` + `RDB$CHARACTER_SETS`/`RDB$COLLATIONS` joins; uses `RDB$CHARACTER_LENGTH` for char length, `RDB$VALIDATION_SOURCE` for the check, `RDB$DEFAULT_SOURCE` for the default), `GetDomainUsageAsync(name)` (Used By = `RDB$RELATION_FIELDS WHERE RDB$FIELD_SOURCE = @name` for table/view columns — the primary case, **NOT in RDB$DEPENDENCIES**, cf. #46 — PLUS `RDB$DEPENDENCIES` type 9 for PSQL refs; combined into one flat list). Internal `BuildDomainInfo` mapping helper + `DomainInfoSql`/`DomainUsageColumnsSql`/`DomainUsageDependenciesSql` consts (all pinned by tests). Metadata-lane access, lock captured once (#98/#120). All three SQL strings were executed against a live FB5 with a real `T_ADRES`/`KONTRAHENCI.ADRES` to confirm shape + that Used By yields `Tables → KONTRAHENCI.ADRES`. `FirebirdDdlReader`'s domain placeholder is left untouched (unreachable — `OpensAsDomainDetail` routes every domain open to the Detail surface, whose DDL tab is built from `DdlGenerator.BuildCreateDomain` so it always matches Save).

**App** — [DomainDetailTabViewModel.cs](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/DomainDetailTabViewModel.cs) (numeric form fields are `decimal?` bound to `NumericUpDown`; UPPERCASE name coercion in the New flow per #141; baselines captured on load so Save emits the minimum-set of `ALTER DOMAIN`/`COMMENT` statements; DDL tab recomputed live from the form via `BuildCreateDomain`). `SaveCommand` builds the diff DDL (`BuildSaveSql`, pure + internal) → `FirebirdDdlExecutor` → **autonomous auto-commit (Phase A), so no working transaction and no Commit/Rollback pair on a domain tab** (like Trigger/Generator; excluded from `ShowTransactionButtons`). `RevertChangesCommand` (gated `IsDirty && !IsNew`, confirms) reloads from DB; `DeleteCommand` (gated `!IsNew`, confirms) raises `DeleteRequested` → owner runs `DROP DOMAIN` + closes the tab + refreshes the tree. The New-domain form reuses `AddFieldDialogViewModel.BasicTypes` (data-type list) + `CharsetCatalog.Supported` (charset list) — no second type vocabulary. [DomainDetailTabView.axaml](src/EmberTern.App/Views/DomainDetailTabView.axaml)(.cs) — the form (`field-label` rows, gated controls) + editable description + the single Used By `TreeView` (reuses `BuildDependencyTree` + the dep-tree templates + double-click navigation) + read-only DDL editor (theme swap, no autocomplete). [WorkspaceTabViewModel.cs](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/WorkspaceTabViewModel.cs) — `DomainDetail` enum value + `CreateDomainDetail` factory + property + `UnsavedWork` case.

**Routing/persistence/toolbar** ([MainWindowViewModel.cs](src/EmberTern.App/ViewModels/MainWindowViewModel.cs), [MainWindow.axaml](src/EmberTern.App/Views/MainWindow.axaml)) — strictly parallel to the Generator path: `OpensAsDomainDetail` + `CreateDomainDetail` factory (wires `OpenObjectRequested`/`ConfirmationRequested`/`DeleteRequested`) + `OnOpenDdlRequested` branch + `(Kind,Name)` dedup; `NewDomain` command (`Icon.DomainPlus`, in the New-X toolbar group) + `OnDomainCreated` (refresh tree → close New tab → reopen real domain); `OnDomainDeleteRequested`; `IsDomainDetailTabActive`/`ActiveDomainDetail` + notify chain + `SelectTab` lazy-load + `IsClosableTabActive`; native `CoreTabKind.DomainDetail` persistence (Snapshot skips transient New tabs / Restore lazy-loads + seeds cached DDL); `CanCreateDomain` re-notify on connection change; Save/Revert/Delete editor-toolbar buttons gated on `IsDomainDetailTabActive`; sibling `<views:DomainDetailTabView>`. New strings in [UiStrings.cs](src/EmberTern.App/UiStrings.cs); composed `Icon.DomainPlus` (diamond + bottom-right `+`) in [IconGeometries.axaml](src/EmberTern.App/Themes/IconGeometries.axaml). `Icon.Domain` + `IconColor_Domain` already existed (metadata tree).

**Double-click in the metadata tree** opens Domain Detail (not a DDL tab) — identical to Tables/Views/Procedures/Triggers/Generators, via the shared `OpensAsDomainDetail` predicate in `OnOpenDdlRequested`. Used By navigation reuses `MapObjectTypeToKind`.

**Tests** ([DomainDetailTests.cs](tests/EmberTern.Tests/DomainDetailTests.cs), +55): `OpensAsDomainDetail` predicate; factory (Save/Delete capability); direct-open → DomainDetail tab (not DDL) + dedup; native restore; capture persists as DomainDetail; New command re-notifies on connect; DDL-generator shape (char-with-all-clauses / numeric / blob / plain / skip-NONE / bare-check-wrapped / empty-name throw; SetDefault/DropDefault/AddCheck-normalize/DropConstraint/CommentDomain/DropDomain); `BuildSaveSql` (New CREATE [+COMMENT]; existing no-change → empty / SET DEFAULT / ADD CHECK / COMMENT); dirty tracking + Save/Delete/Revert gating; `DeleteCommand` raises `DeleteRequested` after confirm; `GetUnsavedWork` (clean/new/existing); Used By navigation; reader SQL shape (DomainInfoSql columns, DomainUsageColumnsSql = RDB$RELATION_FIELDS not RDB$DEPENDENCIES, DomainUsageDependenciesSql type 9) + `MapObjectType(9)` + `BuildDomainInfo` mapping (VARCHAR/NUMERIC/BLOB).

**Known limitations / deferred.** ⚠️ SUPERSEDED by the 2026-06-29 follow-up below — type / charset / NOT NULL / rename are now editable on an existing domain (Firebird supports them; only Collation stays read-only). This paragraph's original "out of scope, read-only after create" stance was reversed. Live-app flow against the user's FB3/FB5 remains the user's manual pass; the reader SQL + DDL generation are verified via `isql`.

**Gotcha — promote to architecture lore.**

148. **⚠️ CORRECTED by the 2026-06-29 follow-up — see gotcha #150. Firebird `ALTER DOMAIN` CAN change the type AND the character set (via the `TYPE … CHARACTER SET` form); only COLLATION is unchangeable.** The original claim here ("charset/collation fixed at creation, read-only forever; type/rename out of scope") was wrong/overly-restrictive — re-verified against the lab DB on FB 5.0.3. What remains true: a domain has at most ONE check constraint (unnamed), so changing it is `DROP CONSTRAINT` then `ADD [CONSTRAINT] CHECK (…)`; `SET DEFAULT` both adds and replaces, while `DROP DEFAULT` errors if none exists (so only emit it when a default was present). Table/view columns built on a domain are recorded in `RDB$RELATION_FIELDS.RDB$FIELD_SOURCE`, **NOT** `RDB$DEPENDENCIES` (same class as the FK gotcha #46) — a domain's "Used By" must query `RDB$RELATION_FIELDS` for the primary case and `RDB$DEPENDENCIES` type 9 only for PSQL references.

### Domain & Generator follow-up — Compile rename + ALTER DOMAIN unlock (shipped 2026-06-29)

Tidying sprint, no new object category. Two problems from manual UI testing. **1691 → 1711 tests** (+20). Build 0/0, all green. Verified end-to-end against the lab DB on FB 5.0.3.

**Problem 1 — "Save" → "Compile" (UX consistency).** Generator Detail + Domain Detail used a Save button (`Icon.Save`, "Save generator/domain", "Save failed") while every other editor (Table/View/Procedure/Trigger/Function) uses Compile. Unified both onto the Compile vocabulary so they behave identically and so the word "Save" is free for a future "Save to File": `Icon.Hammer` + "Compile …(CREATE/ALTER …)" tooltip + "Compile failed: {0}". Renamed the VM surface too — `SaveCommand`→`CompileCommand`, `CanSave`→`CanCompile`, `ExecuteSaveAsync`→`ExecuteCompileAsync`, `BuildSaveSql`→`BuildCompileSql` (tests + [MainWindow.axaml](src/EmberTern.App/Views/MainWindow.axaml) updated). No keyboard shortcut added/removed — the other editors' Compile is toolbar-only, so these match (only F5/Ctrl+Enter/Alt+F exist as window keybindings). The unrelated `DialogSave` (connection dialog) and `TableDescriptionSave` (table Opis tab) are different features, not in scope.

**Problem 2 — Domain editing unlocked to match what Firebird actually supports.** The prior milestone locked nearly every field on an existing domain (only Default/Check/Description editable) based on the wrong gotcha #148. Re-verified ALTER DOMAIN on the lab DB (FB 5.0.3) and unlocked everything the engine supports:
- **Type / length / precision / scale / sub-type** → `ALTER DOMAIN "N" TYPE <t>` (the headline fix: VARCHAR(50)→VARCHAR(100)). New `DdlGenerator.BuildAlterDomainType`.
- **Character set** (char types) → `ALTER DOMAIN "N" TYPE <t> CHARACTER SET <cs>` — **the charset IS changeable via the TYPE form** (contradicts old #148). `DdlGenerator.ComposeDomainTypeWithCharset` (now public) builds the `<type>[ CHARACTER SET cs]` clause shared by CREATE and ALTER.
