| **R4** low-selectivity index | index used but reads many rows for few returned | measured **IDX-read amplification** = primary signal; catalog `RDB$STATISTICS` = the *why* | **Phase 3a** |
| **R5** uninitialized stats | `RDB$INDICES.RDB$STATISTICS ≈ 0` | catalog signal; measured bad-path corroboration optional; remedy (**Recompute statistics**) already exists in EmberTern | **Phase 3a** |
| **R6** high read amplification | slow + amplified with **no single culprit scan** (the Szkoleniowa 72× via index reads + 19 sub-queries) | Phase-2 amplification metric; `FindingKind.HighReadAmplification` already defined (unemitted) | **Phase 3a** (DECISION 2: included) |
| **R2** missing index | likely-missing-index *candidate* | R1-costly ∧ high-amp ∧ **sargable** predicate on the scanned column ∧ catalog shows **no usable index** ∧ table not tiny; **finding only, no DDL**, Low/Medium confidence, caveats surfaced | **Phase 3b** (DECISION 3: split — highest false-positive risk, must not block 3a) |

**DECISION 3 (locked) — Phase 3a vs 3b.** **Phase 3a** = catalog reader → predicate extraction → sargability → context+engine → migrate R1 → R4 → R3 → R6 → R5. **Phase 3b** (after 3a is working + validated) = R2 missing-index detection (finding-only; DDL/one-click are a still-later phase).

**Phase 3a implementation order (locked):** (1) `FirebirdCatalogReader` + `CatalogModel`/`TableCatalogInfo`/`IndexModel` + `CatalogCapture` (indexes, segment/leading columns, `RDB$STATISTICS` selectivity, capped cardinality, FB5 partial-index `RDB$CONDITION_SOURCE`; additive `Analyze(capture, catalog?)`). (2) `PredicateExtractor`. (3) `SargabilityClassifier`. (4) `PerformanceContext` + `PerformanceContextBuilder` + `IPerformanceRule` + `PerformanceRuleEngine` + `PerformanceRuleCatalog`; `Finding += RuleId + Confidence`. (5) migrate the measured full-scan finding into **R1**. (6) **R4**. (7) **R3**. (8) **R6**. (9) **R5**. App: enrich `FindingViewModel` with confidence + investigation text — no one-click, no DDL, no UI redesign. **Do NOT start R2, do NOT generate DDL/CREATE INDEX, do NOT implement one-click fixes in this phase.**

**Missing-index (R2, Phase 3b) false-positive gates (for reference):** one-off query (1 execution, no frequency) → cap confidence; small-table scan is optimal → cardinality gate; optimizer chose NATURAL to return most rows → amplification gate (low amp ⇒ suppress); non-sargable predicate → route to R3 not R2; existing partial/expression/composite index → catalog must model it. All five must hold; finding-only.

### Performance Analysis — Phase 3a (Advisor) — SHIPPED + merged to `master` (2026-07-03)

The measured-first advisor. A query's cost is now EXPLAINED (what likely causes it, why it matters, what to investigate) by a rule engine grounded in the Phase-2 measured reads + catalog + a lightweight predicate/sargability analysis. **No recommendations, no DDL, no one-click fixes, no UI redesign** — the existing Findings zone just carries richer, confidence-scored findings. R2 (missing index) is deliberately Phase 3b.

**Commits (on the branch):** `35fd575` catalog reader + model (step 1) · `c98e864` PredicateExtractor + SargabilityClassifier (steps 2–3) · `b55d0bb` rule engine + PerformanceContext + R1 migration (step 4–5) · `1aea3a1` rules R4/R3/R6/R5 · (this) App catalog wiring + Finding confidence UI.

**Architecture (Core pure; the one interface is `IPerformanceRule`):**
- `FirebirdCatalogReader → CatalogModel/TableCatalogInfo/IndexModel` ([FirebirdCatalogReader.cs](src/EmberTern.Firebird/FirebirdCatalogReader.cs)) — per-table indexes (ordered segment columns, unique/primary, active, selectivity, expression, FB5 partial condition) + a row-count estimate from unique-index selectivity (1/sel — no table scan). Metadata lane, capture-lock-once, FB5 `RDB$CONDITION_SOURCE` version-gated. `IndexModel.CoversLeading/IsUsablePlainIndex/IsExpression/IsPartial` are the coverage primitives.
- `PredicateExtractor` + `SargabilityClassifier` ([Core/Sql](src/EmberTern.Core/Sql)) — extract top-level WHERE + JOIN ON predicates (`QueryPredicate`: column/alias→table/operator/rhs/**bare-vs-expression**), reusing `SqlScanHelpers`+`SqlAliasResolver`; **emit nothing when unsure** (OR-joined, subquery, unidentifiable column). Sargability flags only the two unambiguous non-sargable patterns (function-on-column, leading-wildcard LIKE).
- `PerformanceContext` + `PerformanceContextBuilder` + `IPerformanceRule` + `PerformanceRuleEngine` + `PerformanceRuleCatalog` ([Core/Performance](src/EmberTern.Core/Performance)) — the engine runs the rules over the assembled context (measured access + plan + predicates + sargability + catalog) and returns findings ranked severity-then-confidence. `Finding` gained **`RuleId` + `Confidence`** (High/Medium/Low; **no `PerformanceRuleResult`** — findings already flow through the report/UI). `PerformanceReportBuilder`/`PerformanceAnalyzer` take an additive optional `CatalogModel`; report shape + the consistency lead unchanged.
- **App:** `MainWindowViewModel` captures the catalog for the profiled query's measured tables on the metadata lane when the panel builds (best-effort; degrades to measured-reads-only findings). `FindingViewModel` + the panel card surface "High/Medium/Low confidence" (a subtle right-aligned label) — no action buttons.

**The rules (all measured-first, conservative — prefer no finding over a questionable one):**
- **R1 `CostlyFullScan`** (High) — a table read sequentially at a cost worth flagging (measured seq reads + amplification; small/cheap scans not flagged — the measured "72 isn't scary" fix). Adds catalog "% of table scanned" when cardinality is known (never a gate). *Migrated from the Phase-2 `PerformanceFindings` static builder; that class is deleted.*
- **R4 `LowSelectivityIndex`** (Medium) — a purely index-accessed table with high measured index-read amplification, confirmed by the plan→index→catalog chain showing the used index has low selectivity (≥ 0.1). Silent without catalog confirmation.
- **R3 `NonSargablePredicate`** (Medium) — a non-sargable predicate that blocks an **existing** index (catalog `CoversLeading`) while the table was scanned (≥ 500 seq) → a *rewrite* story. Silent when no existing index (that's R2/3b) or the table wasn't scanned.
- **R6 `HighReadAmplification`** (Medium/High) — expensive (≥ 5000 read, ≥ 25×) with **no dominant scan** (sequential < 50% of reads) — the diffuse-cost / many-sub-queries case (the Szkoleniowa 72×). Suppressed when a scan dominates (R1's story). `FindingKind.HighReadAmplification` (was defined-but-unemitted) now fires.
- **R5 `StaleStatistics`** (Low, Medium if corroborated by a seq scan) — an accessed table (≥ 500 reads) has a plain active index with no computed selectivity (Firebird's -1 sentinel). Investigation-oriented ("investigate whether statistics are up to date") — no imperative.

**Language + confidence (locked decision #4):** every finding uses investigation-oriented phrasing ("Likely cause…", "Potential contributor…", "Investigate…") — never "Create index…"/"Change query…". Confidence: R1 High (direct measurement); R4/R3/R6 Medium (inferred plan/predicate link); R5 Low/Medium.

**Tests:** `FirebirdCatalogReaderTests` + `CatalogModelTests` (catalog SQL shape + cardinality + coverage), `PredicateExtractorTests` + `SargabilityClassifierTests` (18), `PerformanceRulesTests` (10 — each rule's fire + silence gates + engine ordering + R1 % scanned). **2248 main + 9 headless probe green.** Build 0/0 (2 pre-existing IndexDetailTabView Watermark warnings). Rule logic is unit-pinned; the live catalog read + end-to-end advisor against a real FB is the user's manual smoke.

**Phase 3b — SHIPPED (see the next milestone):** R2 missing-index detection (finding-only, the five false-positive gates below; still no DDL/one-click). Later phases: CREATE INDEX / recommendations / fix actions, reads-derived plan heat, procedure/function cursor breakdown, trace, performance history.

### Performance Analysis — Phase 3b (Advisor: R2 missing index) — SHIPPED + merged to `master` (2026-07-03)

Completes the advisor stack: **R2 `MissingIndexCandidate`** — the most conservative rule, biased toward silence, finding-only (**no DDL, no CREATE INDEX, no one-click**). Fires only when EVERY gate passes; investigation-oriented, Medium confidence at best.

**Gates (all required):** ① **measured cost** — the table shows a costly sequential scan (measured `SequentialReads ≥ 500`) AND high read amplification (`seq/returned ≥ 20`; low amplification ⇒ the scan returned most rows ⇒ an index wouldn't help ⇒ suppress); ② **sargable + seekable predicate** — a `QueryPredicate` on the table the extractor understood, classified **sargable**, with a seekable operator (`= < <= > >= BETWEEN IN STARTING WITH`; a non-sargable predicate routes to R3, never R2; `<>`/`IS NULL`/`LIKE`/`CONTAINING` excluded); ③ **no usable existing index** — requires the catalog (absent catalog ⇒ silent, can't confirm) and suppresses when any active index covers the column as leading segment (plain OR **partial**) or an **expression** index references it; ④ **table not tiny** — a known `RowCountEstimate < 1000` suppresses. Confidence **Medium** when cardinality confirmed the table isn't tiny, **Low** when cardinality is unknown — never High.

**Language:** "Candidate index opportunity on {table}.{column}" / "Potential contributor: … had no usable index to seek … investigate whether {column}'s selectivity and this query's frequency would justify one. No change is applied here." — never "create/add index".

**Files:** `FindingKind.MissingIndexCandidate` ([Finding.cs](src/EmberTern.Core/Performance/Finding.cs)); [MissingIndexRule.cs](src/EmberTern.Core/Performance/Rules/MissingIndexRule.cs) registered in `PerformanceRuleCatalog`. No App/UI change — it flows through the existing confidence-scored Findings card.

**Tests** ([PerformanceMissingIndexTests.cs](tests/EmberTern.Tests/PerformanceMissingIndexTests.cs), 12): fires (equality + range); every suppression gate — tiny table, plain/partial/expression index coverage, non-sargable, low amplification, non-seekable operator, below seq floor, no catalog; Low confidence when cardinality unknown. **2260 main + 9 headless probe green.** Build 0/0. Rule logic unit-pinned; live end-to-end advisor is the user's manual smoke.

**Phase 3 is now complete** (3a rules R1/R3/R4/R5/R6 + 3b R2). Remaining Performance Analysis roadmap: recommendations / CREATE INDEX / one-click fixes / Test-Index (guarded), reads-derived plan cost heat tint, procedure & function cursor breakdown (FB5 `MON$COMPILED_STATEMENTS`), Services trace upgrade, per-query performance history + regression detection, and (V3/FB6) true cost/cardinality + estimate-vs-actual.

### Performance Analysis — Phase 4 (Actionable Guidance) — SHIPPED + merged to `master` (2026-07-03)

The advisor now answers **"what should I investigate next?"** — each finding carries a concise "What to investigate" block. Still purely advisory: **no CREATE/ALTER INDEX, no executable fixes, no one-click, no schema changes, no AI.**

**Architecture — a pure, kind-keyed guidance layer, zero coupling to the rules.** `FindingGuidance(Heading, Items)` + `FindingGuidanceCatalog.For(FindingKind)` ([FindingGuidance.cs](src/EmberTern.Core/Performance/FindingGuidance.cs)) map each rule's finding kind to 2–3 investigation bullets. Derived from the **kind only**, so it adds nothing to `Finding` or the rules and always matches the finding; guidance **inherits the finding's confidence** (it renders on the same confidence-labelled card). Language is strictly investigation-oriented (Review / Check / Consider / Verify) — a test bans DDL/imperatives (`CREATE INDEX`, `ALTER INDEX`, `ADD INDEX`, `MUST`, `EXECUTE`, `FIX`).

**Guidance per rule:** R1 costly scan → filter selectivity / suitable index exists / table size + access pattern. R2 missing-index → filtered columns + order / existing index coverage / query frequency. R3 non-sargable → expression on the column / can the condition reference the column directly / computed(expression) index appropriate. R4 low-selectivity → value distribution / composite-index leading column / different access path. R5 stale stats → index statistics / up to date. R6 amplification → join breadth / sub-queries / filtering effectiveness.

**UI (no redesign):** `FindingViewModel` exposes `GuidanceHeading`/`GuidanceItems`/`HasGuidance`; the existing finding card in [PerformancePanelView.axaml](src/EmberTern.App/Views/PerformancePanelView.axaml) renders a subtle "What to investigate" heading + bulleted list beneath the evidence — same visual hierarchy, `SubtleForegroundBrush`, no new controls/toolbars.

**Tests** ([FindingGuidanceCatalogTests.cs](tests/EmberTern.Tests/FindingGuidanceCatalogTests.cs), 9): every emitted kind has 2–3 bullets + the "What to investigate" heading; no banned prescriptive phrases anywhere; `FindingViewModel` surfaces the guidance. **2269 main + 9 headless probe green.** Build 0/0. UI is unit/markup-verified; the live rendering is the user's manual smoke.

**Roadmap after Phase 4 (all future phases):** actionable recommendations / CREATE INDEX generation / one-click + guarded Test-Index; reads-derived plan cost-heat tint; procedure & function cursor breakdown (FB5 `MON$COMPILED_STATEMENTS`); Services trace upgrade; per-query performance history + regression detection; V3/FB6 true cost/cardinality + estimate-vs-actual.

### Performance Analysis — Phase 5 (Actionable Recommendations) — SHIPPED + merged to `master` (2026-07-03)

The advisor now answers **"what are the most likely improvements?"** — each finding shows a single "Potential improvement" line beneath its guidance. **Recommendation-ONLY: no database changes, no DDL, no CREATE/ALTER INDEX, no executable SQL, no one-click, no auto-stats-refresh, no AI.**

**Architecture — the third pure layer (`Finding → Guidance → Recommendation`), separate from both.** `Recommendation(Heading, Text)` + `RecommendationCatalog.For(Finding)` ([Recommendation.cs](src/EmberTern.Core/Performance/Recommendation.cs)). It **consumes the finding** (which only exists because a rule's measured/catalog/predicate gates passed) → recommendations are evidence-derived, **never plan-heuristic**. It stays separate from `FindingGuidanceCatalog` (Phase 4). Recommendations **inherit the finding's confidence** (render on the same confidence-labelled card). Language is "Potential improvement / worth reviewing / consider / review whether…" — a test **bans** must / required / guaranteed / fix / create / add index / execute.

**One structured add:** `Finding.Column` (nullable, additive — like `RuleId`/`Confidence`), set by R2 + R3, so the R2 recommendation names the exact column (e.g. "…an index on CUSTOMER_ID…") with a "the filtered column" fallback.

**Recommendation per rule:** R1 → "Review whether an index on the filtered column would reduce this scan." · R2 → "Review whether an index on {Column} would benefit this workload." · R3 → "Review whether the expression can be removed from the indexed column so an index can be used." · R4 → "Review whether a more selective leading column exists for this index." · R5 → "Review whether the index statistics should be refreshed." · R6 → "Review the join breadth and sub-query strategy."

**UI (no redesign):** `FindingViewModel` exposes `RecommendationHeading`/`RecommendationText`/`HasRecommendation`; the existing finding card renders a small "Potential improvement" section (accent heading, foreground text) **beneath** the Phase-4 "What to investigate" block — no new panels/wizards. Card order is now: severity+title+confidence → explanation → evidence → What to investigate → Potential improvement.

**Tests** ([RecommendationCatalogTests.cs](tests/EmberTern.Tests/RecommendationCatalogTests.cs), 10): every emitted kind has a "Potential improvement" recommendation; R2 names the column (fallback when unknown); no banned prescriptive/DDL phrases; `FindingViewModel` surfaces it. **2279 main + 9 headless probe green.** Build 0/0. Live rendering is the user's manual smoke.

**Roadmap after Phase 5 (all future phases):** CREATE INDEX generation / SQL scripts / one-click + guarded Test-Index / automatic stats refresh; reads-derived plan cost-heat tint; procedure & function cursor breakdown (FB5 `MON$COMPILED_STATEMENTS`); Services trace upgrade; per-query performance history + regression detection; V3/FB6 true cost/cardinality + estimate-vs-actual.

### Performance Analysis v1 — COMPLETE (final audit, 2026-07-03)

Phases 1–5 are shipped **and merged to `master`**; **no Performance Analysis phase is in progress.** Final audit against the *Firebird Optimizer* book (Kovyazin, 2025–2026 ed.), the frozen roadmap, and the live architecture:

- **Roadmap complete.** Phase 1 (plan + timings + summary), Phase 2 (MON$ measured reads + amplification + table access), Phase 3 (advisor rules R1–R6 behind `IPerformanceRule`), Phase 4 (per-finding "What to investigate" guidance), Phase 5 (per-finding "Potential improvement" recommendation) — all implemented, tested (**2279 main + 9 headless probe green**), TWAE clean.
- **Rules R1–R6 all still valid, none redundant.** R1 costly scan · R2 missing-index candidate · R3 non-sargable predicate · R4 low-selectivity index · R5 stale statistics · R6 high read amplification — each maps to a book chapter (Reads/access-methods/transformations/selectivity/gstat) and none is covered by another (R2 vs R3 correctly split index-vs-rewrite; R6 suppressed when R1 dominates).
- **Advisor quality: "prefer silence over false positives" upheld.** Every rule is measured-first and emits nothing when the measured/catalog/predicate evidence doesn't confirm the cause; language is investigation-oriented (test-enforced). One noted, strictly-optional micro-refinement (R1's Low tier can flag a small full-return scan — Low severity, mitigated by "% of table scanned"); NOT implemented, not a defect.
- **Architecture clean, no drift (Phases 1–5).** Core references neither Avalonia nor FirebirdSql (grep-verified); Firebird holds all `Fb*` and returns Core DTOs; App is presentation-only (string keys, no Avalonia types in VMs). One justified interface (`IPerformanceRule`); all `Analyze`/`Finding` extensions additive.
- **No blocking gap found.** The book chapters without advisor coverage — external **Sorts** (Ch 9), **Groupings** (Ch 10), dedicated **Join-algorithm/order** cost (Ch 8) — require data FB3–5 don't expose to a non-privileged reader: external-sort cost is CPU/memory/temp-file (not in per-table `Reads`; needs Services-trace/MON$-memory), and join-order needs plan **cost/cardinality** (FB6-only). Adding them now could only be plan-heuristic, which breaks the measured-first principle. They are correctly reserved for the future trace + FB6-cost phases. (Joins are already *transitively* covered: an expensive join surfaces as R1 seq reads on the inner table or R6 amplification.)
- **Live-usage gaps → next module (not Performance Analysis v1):** execution metrics for `EXECUTE PROCEDURE`/`EXECUTE BLOCK`/DML, INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE counts from `MON$RECORD_STATS`, richer + selectable/copyable Messages, non-result-set Performance summaries. Designed + approved; tracked on branch `feat/execution-metrics` (see the "Execution Metrics" plan below). This is a **separate** module built on the Performance foundation, not a reopening of v1.

Findings carry RuleId + High/Medium/Low **confidence** with investigation-oriented language; **no recommendations/DDL/CREATE INDEX/one-click/UI-redesign**. Build 0/0, **2260 + 9 tests green**. See the "Phase 3a/3b (Advisor) — SHIPPED" milestones. **Phase 4 (Actionable Guidance) COMPLETE + merged to `master` (2026-07-03)** — each finding carries a concise, confidence-inherited "What to investigate" block via a pure kind-keyed `FindingGuidanceCatalog`; still advisory-only (no DDL/fixes/one-click/AI); no UI redesign; **2269 + 9 tests green**. See the "Phase 5 (Actionable Recommendations) — SHIPPED" milestone. **✅ Performance Analysis v1 — COMPLETE (2026-07-03): all of Phase 1–5 shipped + merged to `master`, no phase in progress.** Final audit (against the *Firebird Optimizer* book + the frozen roadmap): all six rules R1–R6 still valid, none redundant; architecture clean (Core pure, Firebird isolated, App presentation, no drift); advisor upholds "prefer silence over false positives". The uncovered book chapters (Sorts/Groupings/Joins-cost) require **measured** sort-memory / plan-cost data that FB3–5 don't expose, so they're correctly deferred to the future trace + FB6-cost phases — implementing them now would break the measured-first principle. See the "Performance Analysis — Phase 6 (Final Audit)" note below. Design frozen — extend, don't redesign.
  - **UX refinement (presentation-only, Core/Firebird/parser/tests unchanged):** the panel is **auto-available after execution (Option B)** — it's a *view* of the last data-lane run, built lazily when the tab is shown (a ~2 ms prepare-only plan re-read via `FirebirdPlanReader`), **no second execution**; `MainWindowViewModel` stores the last run (`_lastProfiledSql`/`_lastProfiledResult`) + `Performance.MarkStale()` on execute, `OnSelectedBottomTabIndexChanged` → `SetVisible`, and `BuildPerformanceFromLastRunAsync` assembles the `PerformanceCapture` from the real `QueryResult` (no re-run); "Run & profile" → **Refresh** (re-reads the plan, never re-runs). `PerformanceProfiler` (Firebird) retained but no longer instantiated. **Information hierarchy:** a plain-language summary is primary (`PerformanceInsight` derives, from the parsed plan, "It reads table NAGL in full…", "It also evaluates 22 sub-queries", an honest Phase-2 forward-pointer); the execution plan is **demoted** into a collapsed "advanced" section (auto-expands the path to each full scan, collapses inert sub-query branches, full-scan nodes in `WarningBrush`); Details folded in (raw plan + capture/form); timing promoted to the summary. Findings + table-access are subtle Phase-2 placeholders. Gotcha: a TreeViewItem `IsExpanded` style-setter binds against the host DataType, so it needs `ReflectionBinding` (gotcha #38).
  - Original Phase 1 (unchanged): Shipped: Core `EmberTern.Core/Performance/` (PlanTree/PlanNode + enums, StatementIdentity, ExecutionTimings, RawPlanCapture, PerformanceCapture, PerformanceReport; `PlanParser` Explain+Legacy tolerant; `PerformanceReportBuilder`+`PerformanceAnalyzer`); Firebird `FirebirdPlanReader` (driver `GetCommandExplainedPlanAsync` FB3+, Legacy fallback, lock-once, best-effort→null) + `PerformanceProfiler` (execute-first, plan best-effort, Core DTO out) + `PerformanceCaptureException`; App `PerformancePanelViewModel`+`Verdict`/`PlanNode`/`ExecutionDetails` VMs + `PerformancePanelView` (4-zone: verdict / findings-placeholder / access-placeholder+plan-tree / details) as a new SQL-editor **bottom-panel `Performance` sub-tab**, wired in `MainWindowViewModel` via `ProfileActiveQueryAsync` ("Run & profile" executes the active query under the user's manual data tx). Verdict grade is a conservative **time-based** proxy; a full scan is a **neutral fact**, never an alarm (no per-table reads yet). Build 0 errors (only the 2 pre-existing IndexDetailTabView Watermark warnings); **2201 tests green** (2192 + 9 probe; +24 new: `PlanParserTests` 13, `PerformanceReportTests` 11); 8s smoke clean. **Implementation decisions:** (a) profiling is opt-in "Run & profile" that executes independently and feeds only the Performance panel (zero impact on the F5 grid path, no executor change); (b) `PerformanceAnalyzer.Analyze(capture)` is single-arg in Phase 1 — Phase 2 adds an optional `CatalogCapture` param (additive, not restructuring), so `CatalogCapture`/`PerTableReadRow`/`IoCounters` types are deferred to Phase 2 rather than shipped empty; (c) **no `IPerformanceRule` interface exists yet** (Phase 1 has no rules) — it arrives with the advisor in Phase 2. Still NOT implemented (Phase 2/3): MON$ per-table reads, Trace, advisor/rule engine, missing-index, predicate parser, recommendations, procedure/function breakdown, reads heatmap. Fixes the 10–15 s pre-dialog delay on large schemas (the recompile source-fetch-per-object loop now runs with the dialog on screen). `EMBERTERN_BATCH_DIAG=1` logs the phase breakdown. - **Metadata Tree & Context Menu sprint (shipped 2026-07-01).** Tree is now the primary manage surface: per-category/leaf context menus (New/Edit/Delete/Execute/Activate-Deactivate/Recompile) reusing the existing detail editors + DROP builders; connection-node database-wide ops (Refresh / Recompute all index stats / Recompile all objects) via one batch pipeline + a reusable **BatchResultsDialog**. Fixed: Refresh button now disables with no connection; the editor-toolbar strip collapses when the active tab has no commands; **filter/scroll lag root-caused + fixed** (collection-based filtering instead of `IsVisible`-hiding — gotcha #154). Triggers/indexes show real activate/deactivate + dimmed "(inactive)" styling; proc/func/pkg get Recompile (no fake inactive state). No TreeView multi-select — bulk trigger ops act on the visible (filtered) set or all. Live-DB flow is the user's manual pass. See the milestone above + gotcha #154.
- **Privileges bulk-ops redesign (shipped, awaiting user live-smoke).** Replaced the disliked combobox-then-Grant/Revoke step + the per-header ▾ menu with a **contextual one-click trio** of the same three glyphs everywhere — **✓ grant** (`SuccessIconBrush`) · **✓+ grant + grant option** (`AccentBrush`) · **✕ revoke** (`DangerIconBrush`), matching the tri-state cell vocabulary. *Location encodes scope:* **① toolbar** above the grid = all visible objects; **② inline trio in each column header** (replaces the ▾ flyout) = that privilege over all visible; **③ hover trio in the Object cell** (`StackPanel.row-bulk`, revealed on `DataGridRow:pointerover`/`:selected` — gotcha #35) + the enriched right-click menu (now 3 items) = that object, all privileges. **No new columns, single-cell editing untouched.** One pure `StatementFor(...)` builds each GRANT/REVOKE (predicates: Grant when state 0, GrantWithOption when state<2 i.e. upgrades plain→option, Revoke when state>0), reused by the row/column/global batch paths; same autonomous-tx batch + skip-already-matching + grid reload. Light confirm only on the broadest op (global Revoke all). 9 commands on `SecurityPrivilegesPaneViewModel`/`PrivilegeRowViewModel` (`BulkGrant`/`BulkGrantWithOption`/`BulkRevoke`, `GrantColumn`/`GrantColumnWithOption`/`RevokeColumn`, row `GrantAll`/`GrantAllWithOption`/`RevokeAll`). Build 0/0, 1875 tests; the filtering/popup-free one-click flow against the live FB is the user's manual pass.
- **Security Manager (shipped 2026-06-30, merged to `master`).** Unified workspace tab — Users / Roles / Membership / Privileges (object + column grants, role membership) — for Firebird security. Not a singleton: keyed by the context user/role `(Kind, Name)`; double-click opens-or-focuses with the item auto-selected; a central shield button on the main toolbar (between Connection and Create-Object sections) opens a context-less manager. Immediate-apply (autonomous auto-commit). Privileges use **tri-state cells** (none / granted / granted WITH GRANT OPTION); bulk operations are the **contextual one-click trio** (toolbar = all visible · column-header trio = one privilege · row hover trio + right-click = one object) — see the bullet above — plus a column-level panel for tables. Membership has a **direction switch** (Member of / Members) with a single tri-state cell. FB5 DDL + catalog + `COMMENT ON ROLE/USER` verified empirically (embedded isql); lab seeded with grants/membership. The live GUI flow is the user's manual pass. See the "Security Manager" milestone.
- **Index Detail (shipped 2026-06-30).** Indexes open in a dedicated 2-tab read-mostly surface — Index (properties form: Name / Table / Constraint type / Fields-or-Expression / Unique / Sort direction / Statistics / Active / Description) · DDL — via a focused form-based `IndexDetailTabViewModel` (modeled on Generator; no New flow, no Dependencies tab). A Firebird index is almost entirely read-only (verified on FB 5.0.3, gotcha #153): only **Active/Inactive** (plain indexes only — PK/FK/UNIQUE backing indexes reject it) and **Description** (`COMMENT ON INDEX`) are editable; no rename, no structural ALTER. **Compile** (titlebar `Icon.Hammer`) emits `ALTER INDEX ACTIVE/INACTIVE` + `COMMENT ON INDEX`; **Recompute statistics** (`SET STATISTICS INDEX`), Revert, Delete (gated `!IsConstraintBacked`). Constraint-backed indexes are clearly flagged with a `WarningBrush` note explaining why Active/Drop are disabled. **No standalone New-Index flow** — index creation stays in Table Detail → Indeksy. Double-click in the tree opens it (not DDL). FB5 ALTER/COMMENT/dependency behavior + the reader SQL verified on `Lab/EmberTern_Lab.fdb` (lab extended with standalone index variants). See the "Index Detail" milestone + gotcha #153.
- **Exception Detail (shipped 2026-06-30).** Firebird custom EXCEPTIONs open in a dedicated 4-tab surface — Exception (editable Name + Message form) · Description (editable `COMMENT ON EXCEPTION`) · Dependencies (dual tree — "used by" procedures/functions/triggers/packages) · DDL — via a focused form-based `ExceptionDetailTabViewModel` (NOT a `SourceObjectDetailTabViewModel` subclass; modeled on Generator/Domain). The deliberately-simplest editor: no PSQL body, no parameters, maximal reuse. `Compile` (titlebar `Icon.Hammer`) emits `CREATE`/`ALTER EXCEPTION` (message) + `COMMENT ON EXCEPTION` (description); New Exception (titlebar `Icon.ExceptionPlus`), Revert (reload from DB), Delete (`DROP EXCEPTION`). No rename (Firebird has no `ALTER EXCEPTION … TO`). All DDL auto-commits via the autonomous path (no Commit/Rollback pair, like Trigger/Generator/Domain). Double-click in the tree opens it (not DDL). Dependencies use `RDB$DEPENDENCIES` type 7. FB5 syntax + the full round-trip verified on `Lab/EmberTern_Lab.fdb` (embedded isql). See the "Exception Detail" milestone.
- **Package Detail follow-up (shipped 2026-06-29).** Fixed two manual-test findings: (1) the Description-tab mini-header said "Table description" in every source editor — now per-object ("Package/View/Procedure/Function/Trigger description"; Generator/Domain were already clean); (2) **CRITICAL** — Auto Format corrupted PACKAGE BODY source (dropped the routine/package `END`s → non-compilable). The PSQL formatter now treats a bare leading `FUNCTION`/`PROCEDURE` inside a package body as a subprogram definition (header + recursed `BEGIN…END`), verified by compiling the formatter's actual output on FB5. See the "Package Detail follow-up" milestone + gotcha #152.
- **Package Detail (shipped 2026-06-29).** Packages open in a dedicated 6-tab surface — Package (editable `CREATE OR ALTER PACKAGE` header + Compile) · Body (editable `RECREATE PACKAGE BODY`) · Members (read-only Functions/Procedures tree, double-click → jump into the header/body editor) · Description (editable `COMMENT ON PACKAGE`) · Dependencies (dual tree) · DDL — via a focused `PackageDetailTabViewModel` (NOT a `SourceObjectDetailTabViewModel` subclass — two source artifacts, no Easy mode; modeled on the View VM). A package is ONE logical object: a single **Compile** runs the header first then the body, stopping if the header fails. New Package (titlebar `Icon.PackagePlus`), Revert (reload from DB), Delete (`DROP PACKAGE`), Format/Comment/Uncomment on the active editor. Members from the catalog (no parsing); navigation via a `SqlScanHelpers`-based find (no new parser). All DDL auto-commits via the autonomous path (no Commit/Rollback pair). Firebird storage + every emitted DDL statement verified empirically via isql; the live GUI flow is the user's manual pass. See the "Package Detail" milestone + gotcha #151.
- **Domain & Generator follow-up (shipped 2026-06-29).** Generator Detail + Domain Detail now use **Compile** (not Save) like every other editor — `Icon.Hammer`, "Compile …" tooltip, "Compile failed", `CompileCommand`/`CanCompile`/`ExecuteCompileAsync`/`BuildCompileSql`. **Domain editing unlocked** to match Firebird (re-verified on FB 5.0.3): on an existing domain you can now change type / length / precision / scale / sub-type (`ALTER DOMAIN TYPE`), character set for char types (`TYPE … CHARACTER SET`), NOT NULL (`SET/DROP NOT NULL`), Default, Check, and the name (`TO` → tab closes + reopens). **Only Collation stays read-only on an existing domain** — Firebird has no ALTER syntax for it. FB rejects data-unsafe TYPE changes (narrowing, index-bound) server-side → surfaces as the editor's error. See the "Domain & Generator follow-up" milestone + gotcha #150 (supersedes #148).
- **Domain Detail (shipped 2026-06-29).** Domains open in a dedicated 4-tab surface — Domain (the definition form: Name / Data type / Length / Precision / Scale / Sub type / Character set / Collation / Default / Check / Not null) · Description (editable `COMMENT ON DOMAIN`) · Used By (a SINGLE dependency tree of the table/view columns + PSQL objects that use the domain) · DDL — via a focused form-based `DomainDetailTabViewModel` (NOT a `SourceObjectDetailTabViewModel` subclass; modeled on the Generator VM). **Editable scope (after the 2026-06-29 follow-up) = type/length/precision/scale/sub-type + charset (char) + NOT NULL + Default + Check + name (rename) + Description**; only **Collation** is read-only on an existing domain (no FB ALTER syntax). `Compile` (titlebar `Icon.Hammer`) assembles the minimum-set of ALTER statements; New Domain (titlebar `Icon.DomainPlus`), Revert (reload from DB), Delete (`DROP DOMAIN`). All DDL auto-commits via the autonomous path (no Commit/Rollback pair, like Trigger/Generator). Double-click in the tree opens it (not DDL). Used By reuses `BuildDependencyTree`. See the "Domain Detail" + "Domain & Generator follow-up" milestones + gotcha #150 (ALTER DOMAIN matrix) / #148-corrected.
- **Generator Detail (shipped 2026-06-29, + bugfix sprint + Compile rename).** Generators open in a dedicated 3-tab surface — Generator (editable form: Name / Current value / Initial value / Increment / Description) · Dependencies · DDL — via a focused form-based `GeneratorDetailTabViewModel` (NOT a `SourceObjectDetailTabViewModel` subclass — a generator has no PSQL body). The main action is **Compile** (titlebar `Icon.Hammer`, was Save). **New flow** emits the definition only (`CREATE SEQUENCE [START WITH initial] [INCREMENT BY inc]` + `COMMENT`); **Current Value edit** on an existing generator emits the version-independent `SET GENERATOR … TO` (NOT `RESTART WITH`, which is off-by-one on FB5); Initial/Increment are editable only at creation, Current Value only on an existing generator (definition vs runtime de-conflated). All DDL auto-commits via the autonomous path (no Commit/Rollback pair, like Trigger). New Generator (titlebar `Icon.GeneratorPlus`), Revert (reload from DB), Delete (DROP SEQUENCE), **Refresh Current Value** (re-reads only `GEN_ID(,0)`, no full reload/no dirty flip). After creation, **Current Value equals Initial Value** on both FB3/FB5 (the New flow pins the counter via `SET GENERATOR` after `CREATE … START WITH`); a negative pre-first-use counter is displayed as 0. Double-click in the tree opens it (not DDL). Semantics verified on real FB3 + FB5. See the "Generator Detail" milestone + the "bugfix sprint" milestone + gotchas #146 (FB2.5 catalog gate) / #147 (FB3-vs-FB4 START WITH/RESTART semantics). Live-app flow is the user's manual pass.
- **Function Editor (shipped 2026-06-29, merged to `master`).** Functions open in a dedicated editor mirroring Procedure (Source⇄Easy, Arguments / single-record Result + Deterministic / Variables / Cursors / Subprograms, Execute on the Data lane → separate Execute Result tab, Description / Dependencies / DDL). Built on the new shared `SourceObjectDetailTabViewModel` base (Procedure + Trigger refactored onto it, no behavior change). Etap 6 (live-FB smoke: open function → Source/Easy round-trip → edit args/result/body → Compile → Execute → New Function → restart/persistence) is the user's manual pass. See the "Function Editor + SourceObjectDetailTabViewModel base" milestone + gotcha #145 (Result ≠ Execute Result). Known limitations: domain-typed args/return show their *resolved* type, not the domain name (mirrors procedures); UDF support is minimal (graceful parse-fail, no special read-only gating — deferred).
- **Revert / Discard on every object editor (shipped 2026-06-20).** The Table designer's discard-pending button now has siblings on the View / Procedure / Trigger source editors (a `RevertChangesCommand` = reload-from-DB, gated `IsDirty && !IsNew`), and **all four — including the Table discard — now confirm before discarding** so an accidental click can't lose uncompiled work. The Revert/Discard button is part of the **editor contract** (mandatory for future Function/Package editors) — see the editor-contract bullet + gotcha #143.
- **SearchableComboBox (Faza 1+2+3 shipped; awaiting user live-smoke) — the app's ONE standard picker for large dictionaries** (`SearchablePicker` deleted): dedicated `TemplatedControl` (chevron + top-level non-clipped popup + in-popup filter + rich multi-column list with header + ✕ clear, **commit only on click/Enter**, empty=empty, `Sections` tabs API for Domain|Table-column). **Type is a plain ComboBox** (small closed list); **Domain is a SearchableComboBox** in all 4 places (FieldGridColumns Procedure/Trigger, New Table, Table Detail Pola) **+ AddFieldDialog / FK ref-table / Trigger header Table** all consolidated onto it. `"(none)"` sentinel removed (clear via ✕). Domain list is now rich: **Name | Type | Size | Scale | Not Null | Charset** with a column header (shared `DomainRowTemplate`/`DomainHeaderTemplate`); `DomainSpec` carries NotNull/Charset + computed BaseType/Size/Scale; reader reads `RDB$NULL_FLAG` + charset. "Type blank after picking a domain" regression fixed (gotcha #142). **Faza 3 done**: picking a domain mirrors its resolved Type/Size/Scale (+SubType/Charset/NotNull for Procedure/Trigger) into the disabled cells, DDL-safe (domain drives the ALTER); changing type away from a size-type clears stale Size/Scale (user-reported bug). **Faza 4 COMPLETE (Krok 1 + Krok 3) — full parity across all 4 editors** (Procedure, Trigger, New Table, Table Detail): the separate Domain + TYPE OF columns are merged into ONE two-tab "Domena/Kolumna" picker (rich Domain list + two-pane table→column picker for `TYPE OF COLUMN`). One shared `ITypeSourceRow` + `MergedTypeSourceColumn` builder + `SelectedTypeSource`/`TypeSourceDisplay` dispatch (DomainSpec→DomainName / ColumnRef→TypeOf / null→clear, mutually exclusive); DDL via `FormatTypeOrDomain` (`TYPE OF COLUMN …`). Section/picker data set imperatively from the row (sections aren't in the visual tree); the table grids swap the XAML Domain column for the code-built merged column in code-behind. **Live-DB verified (2026-06-28)**: a hard crash and a flicker/loop on setting a domain on a table were fixed in the merged picker — both were the popup `TabControl.SelectionChanged` handler catching the inner domain-`ListBox`'s **bubbling** SelectionChanged and re-applying the filter (synchronously → "change source while update in progress" crash; then, after deferring, an async bubbling loop). Fixed by reacting only to the TabControl's own tab switch (`ReferenceEquals(e.Source, tabs)`) + deferring the re-filter (gotcha #144); same `ItemsSource`-during-selection hazard also fixed in `TableColumnPicker.OnTableSelected`. User confirmed working. See the "SearchableComboBox — crash + flicker fixes" milestone.
- **Trigger Editor — pre-merge fixes applied**: PSQL-aware DDL split (the DECLARE-before-BEGIN compile blocker is fixed — gotcha #140); `NEW.`/`OLD.` complete the trigger's table columns; shared `SearchablePicker` (filterable) on the big-list standalone pickers; trigger auto-name `STANMAG_BU99`; UPPERCASE name policy across Trigger/Procedure/View name fields (gotcha #141); trigger toolbar has no transaction buttons. Still on `feat/trigger-editor` (not merged). DB compile/exec flows are user-verified.
- **Trigger Editor**: triggers open in a dedicated **Trigger Detail** 4-tab surface (Editor Source⇄Easy + Compile · Description · Dependencies · DDL). Easy mode = trigger metadata header (Table searchable picker / Timing / Events / Position / Active) + editable Variables grid over a body editor; auto-derived name until overridden; New Trigger toolbar action. Reuses the procedure body splitter/generator + the shared field-grid columns + `SqlFormatter` + dependency trees; trigger-specific header via `TriggerSignatureParser` + `DdlGenerator.BuildCreateOrAlterTrigger`. On branch `feat/trigger-editor` (not merged). See the "Trigger Editor (Easy mode)" milestone.
- **Object Explorer (metadata tree)**: lazy **COUNT-only load on connect** (category labels show `Tables (2356)` etc. immediately; full leaf lists load on first expand). **Filter** (debounce ~350 ms) is IBExpert-style: shows per-category **match counts** (`Views (1)`) and **hides zero-match categories**, never auto-expands anything (counts for un-expanded categories come from a session object-name cache). **Type-to-filter** (replaced the IBExpert-style type-ahead, which was removed at the user's request): with the tree focused, typing redirects the character into the filter box and moves focus there (subsequent keys land in the box natively); **Ctrl+F** focuses the filter; **Escape** clears it and returns focus to a tree item. The filter still uses the lazily-built session name cache (strings, not VMs; invalidated on disconnect/reload/refresh). Still on Avalonia `TreeView` + nested `VirtualizingStackPanel`; the residual scroll-extent jump on very large expanded categories is a control limitation — `TreeDataGrid` migration is parked for a future sprint (see the "Object Explorer performance — Etap 1" milestone + its follow-up). `EMBERTERN_PERF_DIAG=1` logs connect/expand timings + heap to `%TEMP%\EmberTern-debug.log` for before/after measurement.
- **UI-state persistence**: per-tab editor state (View/Procedure Source⇄Easy, all three detail editors' active sub-tab, Procedure Easy-collection sub-tab, Table Constraints sub-tab + Grid Edit) survives restart via per-tab `WorkspaceTab` fields under a hybrid model (global preference seeds new objects, per-tab value restores reopened tabs); plus globals `ResultsMaximized` + bottom-panel tab. `AppearanceSettings` removed as dead code. See the "UI-state persistence tightening" milestone for the Capture/Restore symmetry audit + the deliberately-deferred list.
- **Data-loss protection**: Increment 1 (WorkGuard + ChoiceDialog) shipped — tab close / disconnect / app close all route through one guard; no silent loss of uncompiled code or an active transaction. Increment 2 (auto-draft to `drafts.dat`, crash-safe, with schema-validation + version warnings + "Recovered N draft(s)" toast + `●` marker) is designed + approved but **not yet built** — see the milestone above.
- **Icons**: central themeable **SVG system** (Lucide) — `SvgIcon` control + `IconGeometries.axaml` (`Icon.*` `StreamGeometry` keys) + `IconGeometryConverter` (shape) / `IconBrushConverter` (color); VMs hold string keys only. ZERO Unicode/emoji glyph icons remain in any view (metadata tree, tabs, Table Detail, dependency views, toolbars, window caption). `.svg` sources are repo-only (build-excluded). Add a new icon: drop the Lucide `.svg` in `Assets/Icons/<cat>/`, add its `<StreamGeometry x:Key="Icon.Name">`, reuse before create — see [ICONS.md](src/EmberTern.App/Assets/Icons/ICONS.md).
- **UI / theming**: every color flows through the two-file central system ([Colors.axaml](src/EmberTern.App/Themes/Colors.axaml) tokens + [ControlStyles.axaml](src/EmberTern.App/Themes/ControlStyles.axaml) shared styles) — zero hardcoded/local colors in views or code-behind. FluentTheme's `SystemAccentColor` is overridden to the EmberTern blue (top-level, both themes) so checkboxes/radios/combobox selection no longer leak the Win11 amber. Sidebar is resizable (220–600px) + fully collapsible (0px column + left-edge grab rail), and the SQL editor's results panel is height-resizable with 3-state column sort + client-side pagination; sidebar width/collapse + results height persist in `WorkspaceState`. New-UI rules ("Reuse before create" + "UI Review Checklist") live in the UI styling section above.
- **Security / settings**: all user settings live in **one whole-file DPAPI-encrypted file** `%AppData%\EmberTern\settings.dat` (`ApplicationSettings` aggregate: Connections + Folders + Workspace + UserSettings), now wrapped in a **plaintext container header** `EMBERTERN-SETTINGS<TAB>containerVersion<TAB>encryptionScheme\n` ([SettingsFileContainer](src/EmberTern.Core/Settings/SettingsFileContainer.cs)) so a load can identify the file, pick the right protector (`EncryptionSchemes` — `dpapi` today, future AES/passphrase registered in `ResolveProtector`), and reject a newer container/scheme/SchemaVersion without decrypting. `ConnectionProfileStore` / `FolderStore` / `WorkspaceStore` are section facades over a shared `ApplicationSettingsStore` (read-modify-write per section). **Downgrade protection** on three axes guards both `Load` (degrade to null + `LastLoadDiagnostic`) and `Save` (refuse to overwrite a future file + `LastSaveDiagnostic`). **Migration ladder** `MigrateToCurrentVersion` (`Migrate_1_2` + stepwise `switch` — add `Migrate_2_3` etc.) replaces ad-hoc shim migration. Legacy headerless `settings.dat` is read unchanged and re-wrapped with the container on first save (verified on the real file). First launch still migrates legacy `connections.json` / `workspace.json` / `folders.json` (v0 or v1) into `settings.dat` and deletes them. `UserSettings.GridProfiles` is now wired to live grids (column order/width/auto-fit — see the Grid layout profiles milestone); `AppearanceSettings` is still a persisted stub. DPAPI ciphertext is per-user/per-machine → not portable; cross-machine export will register an `aes256-passphrase` `SecretProtector` (seam ready — `ResolveProtector` returns null for unknown schemes today).
- **App**: builds + launches cleanly (8-second-uptime smoke). Table editor: Pola (inline edit + Add/Drop/Move field + Drop FK; PK detected from the PRIMARY KEY constraint, not the per-field flag), Ograniczenia (Add/Drop PK/FK/Check/Unique, PK/Unique with optional USING index config), Indeksy (Add/Drop index + constraint-backed-index guard + **Recompute statistics / all** via an autonomous auto-committed admin tx; `-1` sentinel rendered blank), Dane (paged inline edit + **Set NULL** cell context menu), Opis (editable + Save/Clear), DDL. **Views** open in a dedicated View Detail surface (SQL editable source + Compile · Fields · Dependencies · Data · Description · DDL); New View seeds a `CREATE VIEW` template (SQL-only, no designer). **Procedures** open in a dedicated Procedure Detail surface — **Source ⇄ Easy mode** Editor (Easy = editable Input/Output param grids + read-only Variables/Cursors/Subprograms above a body-only editor; Source = full `CREATE OR ALTER PROCEDURE` text) · Description · Dependencies · DDL · Result (with First/Prev/Next/Last paging) + a persistent execution-info bar (time/rows/affected/error, even for no-result-set procs); toolbar has Compile · **Execute** (Data-lane, parameterized, green) · **Format** (the shared PSQL-aware `SqlFormatter`) · Comment Body (blue) / Uncomment Body (red, block `/* */` around the outer BEGIN…END). Last-used Source/Easy mode persists across procedures + restarts. New Procedure seeds a template (SQL-only, no designer). Computed By has a complete dependency model enforced in the UI (New Table grid cells DISABLE per-row, not just the DDL); domain selection governs/shows the type. New Table grid scrolls horizontally to all columns. Validation/compile messages always surface. Post-data-commit refresh is data-only (no structure reload → no freeze, no spurious "no PK").
- **Transactions (Krok 1 + Krok 2 shipped — TPB profiles retired)**: one DATA connection (#1: SQL, preview/edit, Execute Procedure) + one METADATA connection (#2: browsing). **DDL/Compile runs on the MAIN (data) connection** (co-location — Krok 1) so altering a just-executed procedure no longer hits "object is in use"; it auto-commits in its own tx and requires the data working tx settled first (gotchas #89/#122). **The per-lane TPB profile pickers are gone** — replaced by a single per-connection **Developer Mode** switch: Standard → DDL `NOWAIT` (fail-fast); Developer → DDL `WAIT` + 10s lock timeout (edit objects in use by other sessions — gotcha #123). Data ops + both working-tx lanes are always `read_committed + rec_version + nowait + write`; only the DDL path's wait policy changes. Green **DEV MODE** title-bar badge when on (theme-aware text). SQL-Editor F5 still auto-routes the lane via `SqlStatementClassifier`; one unified Commit / Rollback pair. `EMBERTERN_TX_DIAG=1` logs real server-side TPB. *(Vestigial, ignored, future refactor: `TransactionProfile` enum/catalog + `Data/MetadataTransactionProfile` fields. Kept as-is: metadata attachment, degraded-mode aliasing, lane architecture. Raw F5 DDL in the SQL Editor is not under Developer Mode — stays NOWAIT.)*
- **Branch state**: **`master`** holds all completed + verified work. **Latest (2026-06-30):** the **Index Detail** milestone (dedicated 2-tab read-mostly index editor — Index form + DDL; Active/Inactive + Description editable, constraint-backed indexes flagged + locked; Recompute statistics; no New-Index flow) on branch `feat/index-detail`; **1924/1924 tests** (+49), build 0/0, 12s smoke clean. FB5 `ALTER INDEX`/`COMMENT ON INDEX`/rename/dependency behavior verified empirically via embedded `isql`; the lab DB extended with standalone index variants + `IndexDetailSql` verified against the rebuild. The live GUI click-through is the user's manual pass. **Earlier merge (2026-06-30, `0a33093`):** the **Privileges bulk-actions redesign** — contextual one-click trio (✓ grant · ✓+ grant + option · ✕ revoke) at three scopes (toolbar = all visible · column-header trio = one privilege · row hover trio + 3-item right-click = one object); replaces the bulk combobox + per-header ▾ menu; no new columns, single-cell editing untouched; one pure `StatementFor` shared by all 9 commands; light confirm only on global Revoke all. Committed on `feat/security-manager`, **fast-forwarded into `master`** and pushed to `origin`; **1875/1875 tests**, build 0/0. **Live-smoke PASSED on a real DB** (cell/row/column/global + WITH GRANT OPTION + membership + filters) — the **Security Manager milestone is COMPLETE**. `feat/security-manager` retained. This delivered the previously-deferred "IBExpert-style quick grant/revoke" affordance. **Earlier (2026-06-30, `ddce80c`):** the **Security Manager** base (unified Users / Roles / Membership / Privileges tab) + its post-live-test polish (tri-state privileges, column-level panel, full-name headers, toolbar placement, Membership direction switch) — committed on `feat/security-manager`, **merged into `master`** and pushed; FB5 DDL/catalog verified via embedded `isql`. **Earlier — Latest commit (2026-06-29):** the **Package Detail** milestone (dedicated 6-tab package editor — Package header / Body / Members / Description / Dependencies / DDL; single Compile header→body; members from catalog) **+ its follow-up** (per-object Description labels; the critical PACKAGE BODY formatter fix — gotcha #152) committed on `feat/package-detail` (`bc7bd53`), **fast-forwarded into `master`** and pushed to `origin`. **1769/1769 tests**, build 0/0, smoke clean; Firebird storage + emitted DDL + the formatter's actual output verified on FB5 via `isql`. `feat/package-detail` retained.
